    def process_deposits(self):
        """2100-PROCESS-DEPOSITS."""
        print("PROCESSING DEPOSITS...")  # DISPLAY "PROCESSING DEPOSITS..."
        self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'  # SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
        while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
            # READ ACCOUNT-MASTER NEXT
            #    AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            #    NOT AT END
            #        PERFORM 2110-VALIDATE-DEPOSIT
            #        IF WS-VALID
            #            PERFORM 2120-POST-DEPOSIT
            #            PERFORM 2130-UPDATE-BALANCE
            #            ADD 1 TO WS-TRAN-COUNT
            #        END-IF
            # END-READ
            # Mock reading an account record
            account_record = AccountRecord("1234567890123456", "CUST00000001", "CH", Decimal("1000.00"), Decimal("1000.00"), Decimal("0.00"), Decimal("0.00"), 20230101, 20230101, "A", Decimal("1000.00"), Decimal("12.00"), Decimal("100.00"))

            if account_record:
                self.validate_deposit(account_record)  # PERFORM 2110-VALIDATE-DEPOSIT
                if self.ws_valid_flag == 'Y':  # IF WS-VALID
                    self

class MegaEnterpriseSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.WS_FOUND: bool = False
        self.LOAN_DELINQUENT: bool = False
        self.WS_LATE_PAYMENT_FEE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_NOT_EOF: bool = True
        self.WS_EOF: bool = False
        self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_UMBRELLA_RATE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_TOTAL_DIVIDENDS: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_CURRENT_DATE: str = ''
        self.REPORT_LINE: str = ''
        self.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT: str = ''
        self.TRANSACTION_RECORD: str = ''
        self.AUDIT_RECORD: str = ''
        self.WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP: str = ''
        self.WS_TEMP_DATE: str = ''
        self.WS_FORMATTED_DATE: str = ''
        self.WS_VALID: bool = False
        self.WS_INVALID: bool = False
        self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_BRACKET_3_MAX: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_BRACKET_3_RATE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_BRACKET_5_RATE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_CALC_TAX: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
        self.WS_CUST_COUNT: int = 0
        self.WS_ACCT_COUNT: int = 0
        self.WS_TRAN_COUNT: int = 0
        self.WS_LOAN_COUNT: int = 0
        self.WS_ERROR_COUNT: int = 0
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT: str = ''
        self.CUSTOMER_MASTER = []
        self.ACCOUNT_MASTER = []
        self.LOAN_MASTER = []
        self.INSURANCE_MASTER = []
        self.INVESTMENT_MASTER = []
        self.TRANSACTION_LOG = []
        self.AUDIT_TRAIL = []
        self.REPORT_FILE = []
        self.TRAN_TIMESTAMP: str = ""
        self.TRAN_TYPE: str = ""
        self.TRAN_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.TRAN_STATUS: str = ""
        self.ACCT_ID: str = ""
        self.WS_PROCESS_COUNT: int = 0
        self.WS_CALC_RESULT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.CUST_RISK_RATING: str = ""
        self.WS_APPROVED: bool = False
        self.WS_NOT_APPROVED: bool = False
        self.LOAN_LTV_RATIO: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_CALC_FEE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.INV_CURRENT_PRICE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_TEMP_FLAG: str = ""
        self.ACCT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_CREDIT_CARD_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_CALC_INTEREST: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

    def mark_delinquent(self):
        """3420-MARK-DELINQUENT."""
        self.LOAN_DELINQUENT = True

    def assess_late_fee(self):
        """3430-ASSESS-LATE-FEE."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_FEES += self.WS_LATE_PAYMENT_FEE

    def process_collections(self):
        """3500-PROCESS-COLLECTIONS."""
        print("PROCESSING COLLECTIONS...")

    def handle_defaults(self):
        """3600-HANDLE-DEFAULTS."""
        print("HANDLING DEFAULTS...")

    def process_insurance(self):
        """4000-PROCESS-INSURANCE."""
        self.process_policies()
        self.calculate_premiums()
        self.process_claims()
        self.assess_risk()
        self.renew_policies()

    def process_policies(self):
        """4100-PROCESS-POLICIES."""
        print("PROCESSING INSURANCE POLICIES...")

    def calculate_premiums(self):
        """4200-CALCULATE-PREMIUMS."""
        print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        while not self.WS_EOF:
            insurance_record = self.read_insurance_master()
            if insurance_record is None:
                self.WS_EOF = True
            else:
                self.determine_base_premium(insurance_record)
                self.apply_risk_factor(insurance_record)
                self.calculate_final_premium(insurance_record)

    def read_insurance_master(self) -> InsuranceMaster | None:
        """ Dummy function to simulate reading a record. """
        if self.INSURANCE_MASTER:
            return self.INSURANCE_MASTER.pop(0)
        return None

    def determine_base_premium(self, ins: InsuranceMaster):
        """4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM."""
        match True:
            case ins.INS_LIFE:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = (ins.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000) * self.WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
            case ins.INS_HEALTH:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
            case ins.INS_AUTO:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
            case ins.INS_HOME:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = (ins.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000) * self.WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
            case ins.INS_UMBRELLA:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_UMBRELLA_RATE
            case _:
                pass

    def apply_risk_factor(self, ins: InsuranceMaster):
        """4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR."""
        if ins.INS_CLAIMS_COUNT > 2:
            self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal('1.25')

    def calculate_final_premium(self, ins: InsuranceMaster):
        """4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM."""
        ins.INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        self.WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

    def process_claims(self):
        """4300-PROCESS-CLAIMS."""
        print("PROCESSING INSURANCE CLAIMS...")

    def assess_risk(self):
        """4400-ASSESS-RISK."""
        print("ASSESSING INSURANCE RISK...")

    def renew_policies(self):
        """4500-RENEW-POLICIES."""
        print("RENEWING POLICIES...")

    def process_investments(self):
        """5000-PROCESS-INVESTMENTS."""
        self.update_market_prices()
        self.calculate_portfolio_value()
        self.process_trades()
        self.calculate_dividends()
        self.generate_tax_documents()

    def update_market_prices(self):
        """5100-UPDATE-MARKET-PRICES."""
        print("UPDATING MARKET PRICES...")

    def calculate_portfolio_value(self):
        """5200-CALCULATE-PORTFOLIO-VALUE."""
        print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        while not self.WS_EOF:
            investment_record = self.read_investment_master()
            if investment_record is None:
                self.WS_EOF = True
            else:
                self.calculate_position_value(investment_record)
                self.calculate_gain_loss(investment_record)
                self.update_totals(investment_record)

    def read_investment_master(self) -> InvestmentMaster | None:
        """ Dummy function to simulate reading a record. """
        if self.INVESTMENT_MASTER:
            return self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.pop(0)
        return None

    def calculate_position_value(self, inv: InvestmentMaster):
        """5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE."""
        inv.INV_MARKET_VALUE = inv.INV_QUANTITY * inv.INV_CURRENT_PRICE

    def calculate_gain_loss(self, inv: InvestmentMaster):
        """5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS."""
        inv.INV_GAIN_LOSS = inv.INV_MARKET_VALUE - (inv.INV_QUANTITY * inv.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE)

    def update_totals(self, inv: InvestmentMaster):
        """5230-UPDATE-TOTALS."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += inv.INV_MARKET_VALUE

    def process_trades(self):
        """5300-PROCESS-TRADES."""
        print("PROCESSING TRADES...")
        self.process_buy_orders()
        self.process_sell_orders()
        self.settle_trades()

    def process_buy_orders(self):
        """5310-PROCESS-BUY-ORDERS."""
        pass

    def process_sell_orders(self):
        """5320-PROCESS-SELL-ORDERS."""
        pass

    def settle_trades(self):
        """5330-SETTLE-TRADES."""
        pass

    def calculate_dividends(self):
        """5400-CALCULATE-DIVIDENDS."""
        print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        while not self.WS_EOF:
            investment_record = self.read_investment_master()
            if investment_record is None:
                self.WS_EOF = True
            else:
                if investment_record.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE > 0:
                    self.compute_dividend(investment_record)
                    self.post_dividend()

    def compute_dividend(self, inv: InvestmentMaster):
        """5410-COMPUTE-DIVIDEND."""
        self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = inv.INV_MARKET_VALUE * inv.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE / 4

    def post_dividend(self):
        """5420-POST-DIVIDEND."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_DIVIDENDS += self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

    def generate_tax_documents(self):
        """5500-GENERATE-TAX-DOCUMENTS."""

        self.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(self.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS)
        self.REPORT_LINE = f"TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: {self.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT}"

    def write_transaction(self):
        """8100-WRITE-TRANSACTION."""
        self.TRAN_TIMESTAMP = self.WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        self.TRAN_TYPE = 'DEP'
        self.TRAN_AMOUNT = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        self.TRAN_STATUS = 'C'
        self.TRANSACTION_RECORD = "" #write to transaction record

    def write_audit(self):
        """8200-WRITE-AUDIT."""
        self.AUD_TIMESTAMP = self.WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        self.AUDIT_RECORD = "" #write to audit record

    def format_date(self):
        """8300-FORMAT-DATE."""
        self.WS_FORMATTED_DATE = f"{self.WS_TEMP_DATE[:4]}-{self.WS_TEMP_DATE[4:6]}-{self.WS_TEMP_DATE[6:8]}"

    def validate_account(self):
        """8400-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT."""
        self.WS_VALID = True
        if self.ACCT_ID == " ":
            self.WS_INVALID = True

    def calculate_tax(self):
        """8500-CALCULATE-TAX."""
        match True:
            case self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
                self.WS_CALC_TAX = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * self.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
            case self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= self.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
                self.WS_CALC_TAX = (self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * self.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                                   ((self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * self.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
            case self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= self.WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
                self.WS_CALC_TAX = (self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * self.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + \
                                   ((self.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * self.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + \
                                   ((self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - self.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * self.WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
            case _:
                self.WS_CALC_TAX = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * self.WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

    def termination(self):
        """9000-TERMINATION."""
        self.close_files()

    def display_statistics(self):
        """9200-DISPLAY-STATISTICS."""
        print("============================================")
        print("       PROCESSING STATISTICS                ")
        print("============================================")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_CUST_COUNT)
        print(f"CUSTOMERS PROCESSED:    {self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT}")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_ACCT_COUNT)
        print(f"ACCOUNTS PROCESSED:     {self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT}")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_TRAN_COUNT)
        print(f"TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: {self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT}")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_LOAN_COUNT)
        print(f"LOANS PROCESSED:        {self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT}")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_ERROR_COUNT)
        print(f"ERRORS ENCOUNTERED:     {self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT}")
        print("============================================")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(self.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS)
        print(f"TOTAL DEPOSITS:    {self.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT}")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(self.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS)
        print(f"TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: {self.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT}")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(self.WS_TOTAL_INTEREST)
        print(f"TOTAL INTEREST:    {self.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT}")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(self.WS_TOTAL_FEES)
        print(f"TOTAL FEES:        {self.WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT}")
        print("============================================")

    def fraud_detection(self):
        """7000-FRAUD-DETECTION."""
        self.analyze_patterns()
        self.check_velocity()
        self.geographic_analysis()
        self.behavioral_scoring()
        self.alert_generation()

    def analyze_patterns(self):
        """7100-ANALYZE-PATTERNS."""
        print("ANALYZING TRANSACTION PATTERNS...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        while not self.WS_EOF:
            transaction_record = self.read_transaction_log()
            if transaction_record is None:
                self.WS_EOF = True
            else:
                self.check_amount_threshold(transaction_record)
                self.check_frequency()
                self.check_time_pattern()

    def read_transaction_log(self) -> TransactionLog | None:
        """ Dummy function to simulate reading a record. """
        if self.TRANSACTION_LOG:
            return self.TRANSACTION_LOG.pop(0)
        return None

    def check_amount_threshold(self, tran: TransactionLog):
        """7110-CHECK-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD."""
        if tran.TRAN_AMOUNT > 10000:
            self.flag_large_transaction()

    def flag_large_transaction(self):
        """7115-FLAG-LARGE-TRANSACTION."""
        self.WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
        self.write_audit()

    def check_frequency(self):
        """7120-CHECK-FREQUENCY."""
        pass

    def check_time_pattern(self):
        """7130-CHECK-TIME-PATTERN."""
        pass

    def check_velocity(self):
        """7200-CHECK-VELOCITY."""

        """7300-GEOGRAPHIC-ANALYSIS."""
        print("PERFORMING GEOGRAPHIC ANALYSIS...")

    def behavioral_scoring(self):
        """7400-BEHAVIORAL-SCORING."""
        print("CALCULATING BEHAVIORAL SCORES...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        while not self.WS_EOF:
            customer_record = self.read_customer_master()
            if customer_record is None:
                self.WS_EOF = True
            else:
                self.calculate_risk_score(customer_record)
                self.update_customer_profile(customer_record)

    def read_customer_master(self) -> CustomerMaster | None:
        """ Dummy function to simulate reading a record. """
        if self.CUSTOMER_MASTER:
            return self.CUSTOMER_MASTER.pop(0)
        return None

    def calculate_risk_score(self, cust: CustomerMaster):
        """7410-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE."""
        self.WS_CALC_RESULT = Decimal('0')
        if cust.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE < 600:
            self.WS_CALC_RESULT += 30
        if cust.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS > cust.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE:
            self.WS_CALC_RESULT += 20

    def update_customer_profile(self, cust: CustomerMaster):
        """7420-UPDATE-CUSTOMER-PROFILE."""
        if self.WS_CALC_RESULT > 50:
            cust.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'H'
        elif self.WS_CALC_RESULT > 25:
            cust.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'M'
        else:
            cust.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'L'

    def alert_generation(self):
        """7500-ALERT-GENERATION."""
        print("GENERATING FRAUD ALERTS...")

    def compliance_processing(self):
        """7600-COMPLIANCE-PROCESSING."""
        self.aml_screening()

        self.ofac_check()
        self.pep_screening()
        self.sanction_list_check()

    def aml_screening(self):
        """7610-AML-SCREENING."""
        print("PERFORMING AML SCREENING...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        while not self.WS_EOF:
            transaction_record = self.read_transaction_log()
            if transaction_record is None:
                self.WS_EOF = True
            else:
                if transaction_record.TRAN_AMOUNT >= 10000:
                    self.ctr_filing()
                self.structuring_check()

    def ctr_filing(self):

        """7710-AUTHORIZE-TRANSACTION."""
        print("AUTHORIZING CREDIT CARD TRANSACTIONS...")
        self.check_credit_limit()
        self.check_fraud_score()
        self.send_authorization()

    def check_credit_limit(self):
        """7711-CHECK-CREDIT-LIMIT."""
        if self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT > self.ACCT_OVERDRAFT_LIMIT:
            self.WS_NOT_APPROVED = True
        else:
            self.WS_APPROVED = True

    def check_fraud_score(self):
        """7712-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE."""
        pass

    def send_authorization(self):
        """7713-SEND-AUTHORIZATION."""
        if self.WS_APPROVED:
            self.write_transaction()

    def process_settlement(self):
        """7720-PROCESS-SETTLEMENT."""
        print("PROCESSING CREDIT CARD SETTLEMENTS...")

    def calculate_rewards(self):
        """7730-CALCULATE-REWARDS."""
        print("CALCULATING REWARDS POINTS...")
        self.WS_CALC_RESULT = self.TRAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.01')
        self.WS_TOTAL_FEES += self.WS_CALC_RESULT

    def apply_interest(self):
        """7740-APPLY-INTEREST."""
        print("APPLYING CREDIT CARD INTEREST...")
        self.WS_CALC_INTEREST = self.ACCT_BALANCE * self.WS_CREDIT_CARD_RATE / 12
        self.ACCT_BALANCE += self.WS_CALC_INTEREST

    def generate_statements(self):
        """7750-GENERATE-STATEMENTS."""
        print("GENERATING CREDIT CARD STATEMENTS...")

    def mortgage_processing(self):

        """7810-PROCESS-APPLICATIONS."""
        print("PROCESSING MORTGAGE APPLICATIONS...")

    def underwriting(self):
        """7820-UNDERWRITING."""
        print("PERFORMING UNDERWRITING...")
        self.dti_calculation()
        self.ltv_calculation()
        self.credit_analysis()

    def dti_calculation(self):
        """7821-DTI-CALCULATION."""
        self.WS_CALC_RESULT = self.LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT / (self.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 12)
        if self.WS_CALC_RESULT > Decimal('0.43'):
            self.WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

    def ltv_calculation(self):
        """7822-LTV-CALCULATION."""
        self.LOAN_LTV_RATIO = self.LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE / self.LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE
        if self.LOAN_LTV_RATIO > Decimal('0.80'):
            self.WS_CALC_FEE += self.WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT

    def credit_analysis(self):
        """7823-CREDIT-ANALYSIS."""
        if self.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE < 620:
            self.WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

    def appraisal_review(self):
        """7830-APPRAISAL-REVIEW."""
        print("REVIEWING APPRAISALS...")

    def closing_process(self):
        """7840-CLOSING-PROCESS."""
        print("PROCESSING CLOSINGS...")

    def escrow_management(self):
        """7850-ESCROW-MANAGEMENT."""
        print("MANAGING ESCROW ACCOUNTS...")

        self.pay_taxes()
        self.pay_insurance()

    def wealth_management(self):
        """7900-WEALTH-MANAGEMENT."""
        self.portfolio_analysis()
        self.asset_allocation()
        self.rebalancing()
        self.tax_optimization()
        self.estate_planning()

    def portfolio_analysis(self):
        """7910-PORTFOLIO-ANALYSIS."""
        print("ANALYZING PORTFOLIOS...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        while not self.WS_EOF:
            investment_record = self.read_investment_master()
            if investment_record is None:
                self.WS_EOF = True
            else:
                self.calculate_returns(investment_record)
                self.assess_risk(investment_record)
                self.benchmark_comparison()

    def calculate_returns(self, inv: InvestmentMaster):
        """7911-CALCULATE-RETURNS."""
        if inv.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE > 0:
            self.WS_CALC_RESULT = (inv.INV_CURRENT_PRICE - inv.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE) / \
                                   inv.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE * 100

    def assess_risk(self, inv: InvestmentMaster):
        """7912-ASSESS-RISK."""
        match True:
            case inv.INV_STOCKS:
                self.WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'H'
            case inv.INV_BONDS:
                self.WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'L'
            case inv.INV_MUTUAL_FUND:
                self.WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'M'
            case _:
                self.WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'M'

    def benchmark_comparison(self):
        """7913-BENCHMARK-COMPARISON."""
        pass

    def asset_allocation(self):
        """7920-ASSET-ALLOCATION."""
        print("OPTIMIZING ASSET ALLOCATION...")

    def rebalancing(self):
        """7930-REBALANCING."""
        print("REBALANCING PORTFOLIOS...")

    def tax_optimization(self):

    def cash_flow_forecast(self) -> None:
        """8911-CASH-FLOW-FORECAST."""
        self.ws.ws_calc_result = self.ws.ws_total_deposits - self.ws.ws_total_withdrawals
    
    def reserve_requirements(self) -> None:
        """8912-RESERVE-REQUIREMENTS."""
        self.ws.ws_calc_amount = self.ws.ws_total_deposits * Decimal("0.10")

    def customer_segmentation(self) -> None:
        """9310-CUSTOMER-SEGMENTATION."""
        print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
        self.ws.ws_not_eof = True
        while not self.ws.ws_eof:
            self.read_customer_master()
            if not self.ws.ws_eof:
                self.calculate_clv()
                self.assign_segment()
    
    def read_customer_master(self) -> None:
        """READ CUSTOMER-MASTER NEXT"""
        # Simulate reading from a file, setting WS-EOF accordingly.
        # Replace this with actual file reading logic.
        # For demonstration purposes, we set WS-EOF to TRUE after one iteration.
        if not self.ws.ws_not_eof:
            self.ws.ws_eof = True
        else:
            self.ws.ws_not_eof = False
    
    def calculate_clv(self) -> None:
        """9311-CALCULATE-CLV."""
        self.ws.ws_calc_result = (
            (self.cust.cust_total_balance * self.ws.ws_savings_rate) +
            (self.cust.cust_total_loans * self.ws.ws_personal_rate) +
            (self.cust.cust_total_investments * Decimal("0.01"))
        )
    
    def assign_segment(self) -> None:
        """9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT."""
        if self.ws.ws_calc_result > 10000:
            self.ws.ws_temp_code = 'PLATINUM'
        elif self.ws.ws_calc_result > 5000:
            self.ws.ws_temp_code = 'GOLD'
        elif self.ws.ws_calc_result > 1000:
            self.ws.ws_temp_code = 'SILVER'
        else:
            self.ws.ws_temp_code = 'BRONZE'

    def sweep_accounts(self) -> None:
        """9632-SWEEP-ACCOUNTS."""
        if self.cust.acct_balance > self.cust.acct_min_balance:
            self.ws.ws_calc_amount = self.cust.acct_balance - self.cust.acct_min_balance
            self.cust.acct_balance -= self.ws.ws_calc_amount
            self.ws.ws_total_investments += self.ws.ws_calc_amount

    def main_control(self):
        """0000-MAIN-CONTROL."""
        self.initialization()  # PERFORM 1000-INITIALIZATION
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM 2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
            self.process_transactions()
        self.finalization()  # PERFORM 9000-FINALIZATION
        return  # STOP RUN

    def initialization(self):
        """1000-INITIALIZATION."""
        self.WS_WORK_AREAS = None  # INITIALIZE WS-WORK-AREAS
        self.WS_COUNTERS = None    # INITIALIZE WS-COUNTERS
        self.WS_TOTALS = None      # INITIALIZE WS-TOTALS
        self.WS_CURRENT_DATETIME = "20240101" #MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CURRENT-DATETIME (placeholder - implement datetime retrieval if needed)
        self.WS_CURR_YEAR  = "2024" #MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR  TO RPT-YEAR
        self.WS_CURR_MONTH = "01" #MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO RPT-MONTH
        self.WS_CURR_DAY   = "01" #MOVE WS-CURR-DAY   TO RPT-DAY
        self.RPT_YEAR = self.WS_CURR_YEAR
        self.RPT_MONTH = self.WS_CURR_MONTH
        self.RPT_DAY = self.WS_CURR_DAY
        self.open_files()  # PERFORM 1100-OPEN-FILES
        self.read_parameters()  # PERFORM 1200-READ-PARAMETERS
        self.initialize_tables()  # PERFORM 1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES
        self.load_reference_data()  # PERFORM 1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA

    def open_files(self):
        """1100-OPEN-FILES."""
        self.CUSTOMER_FILE = None #OPEN INPUT  CUSTOMER-FILE (replace with actual file opening logic)
        self.ACCOUNT_FILE = None #OPEN INPUT  ACCOUNT-FILE (replace with actual file opening logic)
        self.TRANSACTION_FILE = None #OPEN INPUT  TRANSACTION-FILE (replace with actual file opening logic)
        self.REPORT_FILE = None #OPEN OUTPUT REPORT-FILE (replace with actual file opening logic)
        self.ERROR_FILE = None #OPEN OUTPUT ERROR-FILE (replace with actual file opening logic)
        self.MASTER_FILE = None #OPEN I-O    MASTER-FILE (replace with actual file opening logic)

        if self.WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':  # IF WS-FILE-STATUS NOT = '00'
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'  # MOVE 'FILE OPEN ERROR' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            self.abort_process()  # PERFORM 9500-ABORT-PROCESS

    def read_parameters(self):
        """1200-READ-PARAMETERS."""
        self.WS_PARAM_DATE = "20240101" #ACCEPT WS-PARAM-DATE FROM DATE (placeholder - implement date retrieval if needed)
        self.WS_PARAM_TIME = "12000000" #ACCEPT WS-PARAM-TIME FROM TIME (placeholder - implement time retrieval if needed)
        self.WS_JOB_ID = 'BATCH-001'  # MOVE 'BATCH-001' TO WS-JOB-ID
        self.WS_ENV_TYPE = 'PRODUCTION'  # MOVE 'PRODUCTION' TO WS-ENV-TYPE
        self.WS_PROCESS_DATE = 20240101 #FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PARAM-DATE). (placeholder - implement date conversion if needed)

    def initialize_tables(self):
        """1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES."""
        for self.WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101):  # PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 100
            #INITIALIZE RATE-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX) (assuming initialization is to zero/empty)
            self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1] = RateTableEntry()
            self.RT_RATE[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1] = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
            self.RT_CODE[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1] = ''  # MOVE SPACES TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)

        for self.WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 51):  # PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 50
            #INITIALIZE BRANCH-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX) (assuming initialization is to zero/empty)
            self.BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX-1] = BranchTableEntry()

    def load_reference_data(self):
        """1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA."""
        self.WS_TBL_IDX = 1  # MOVE 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and self.WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y' OR WS-TBL-IDX > 100
            #READ REFERENCE-FILE INTO WS-REF-RECORD (replace with actual file reading)
            self.WS_REF_RECORD = WsRefRecord()
            
            eof = True
            if self.WS_TBL_IDX <=5 :
                eof = False
                self.WS_REF_RECORD.code = "CODE" + str(self.WS_TBL_IDX)
                self.WS_REF_RECORD.rate = Decimal(str(self.WS_TBL_IDX) + ".99")
            else:
                eof = True

            if eof:  # AT END
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            else:  # NOT AT END
                self.RT_CODE[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = self.WS_REF_RECORD.code  # MOVE WS-REF-CODE TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)
                self.RT_RATE[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = self.WS_REF_RECORD.rate  # MOVE WS-REF-RATE TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
                self.WS_TBL_IDX += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX

        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

    def process_transactions(self):
        """2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS."""
        #READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANSACTION-REC (replace with actual file reading)
        self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC = WsTransactionRec()

        #placeholder read of transaction data, for testing purposes
        eof = True
        if self.WS_TRANS_COUNT <= 5:
            eof = False
            self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.txn_account_id = "ACCT" + str(self.WS_TRANS_COUNT)
            self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.txn_amount = Decimal(str(self.WS_TRANS_COUNT) + "00.00")
            self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.txn_type = "D"

        if eof:  # AT END
            self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        else:  # NOT AT END
            self.WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TRANS-COUNT
            self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.txn_account_id
            self.TXN_AMOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.txn_amount
            self.TXN_TYPE = self.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.txn_type
            
            self.validate_transaction()  # PERFORM 2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION
            if self.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
                self.process_by_type()  # PERFORM 2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE
            else:  # ELSE
                self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def validate_transaction(self):
        """2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION."""
        self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VALID-FLAG

        if self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == '' or self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID is None:  # IF TXN-ACCOUNT-ID = SPACES OR LOW-VALUES
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'  # MOVE 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH

        try:
            float(self.TXN_AMOUNT)
        except ValueError:
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'  # MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH

        if self.TXN_TYPE not in ('D', 'W', 'T', 'I'):  # IF TXN-TYPE NOT = 'D' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'W' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'T' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'I'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'  # MOVE 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

        self.validate_account_exists()  # PERFORM 2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS
        self.validate_business_rules()  # PERFORM 2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES

    def validate_account_exists(self):
        """2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID  # MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
        self.search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'  # MOVE 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def validate_business_rules(self):
        """2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES."""
        if self.TXN_TYPE == 'W':  # IF TXN-TYPE = 'W'
            if self.TXN_AMOUNT > self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:  # IF TXN-AMOUNT > WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
                self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
                self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'  # MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

        if self.TXN_AMOUNT > Decimal("1000000"):  # IF TXN-AMOUNT > 1000000
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'  # MOVE 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def process_by_type(self):
        """2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE."""
        match self.TXN_TYPE:  # EVALUATE TXN-TYPE
            case 'D':  # WHEN 'D'
                self.process_deposit()  # PERFORM 2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT
            case 'W':  # WHEN 'W'
                self.process_withdrawal()  # PERFORM 2400-PROCESS-WITHDRAWAL
            case 'T':  # WHEN 'T'
                self.process_transfer()  # PERFORM 2500-PROCESS-TRANSFER
            case 'I':  # WHEN 'I'
                self.process_interest()  # PERFORM 2600-PROCESS-INTEREST
            case _:  # WHEN OTHER
                self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def process_deposit(self):
        """2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.TXN_DESC = 'DEPOSIT'  # MOVE 'DEPOSIT' TO WS-TXN-DESC
        self.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS
        self.WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-DEPOSIT-COUNT
        self.update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

    def update_account(self):
        """2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT."""
        self.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        self.ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = "20240101" #MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ACCT-LAST-UPDATE (placeholder - implement datetime retrieval if needed)
        #REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD (replace with actual file rewriting)

        if self.WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':  # IF WS-FILE-STATUS NOT = '00'
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'UPDATE FAILED'  # MOVE 'UPDATE FAILED' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
            self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def write_audit_trail(self):
        """2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL."""
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD = AuditRecord()  # INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.audit_account = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID #MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO AUDIT-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.audit_amount = self.TXN_AMOUNT  # MOVE TXN-AMOUNT TO AUDIT-AMOUNT
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.audit_type = self.TXN_TYPE  # MOVE TXN-TYPE TO AUDIT-TYPE
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.audit_timestamp = "20240101" #MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO AUDIT-TIMESTAMP (placeholder - implement datetime retrieval if needed)
        self.WS_AUDIT_RECORD.audit_job_id = self.WS_JOB_ID # MOVE WS-JOB-ID TO AUDIT-JOB-ID
        #WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD (replace with actual file writing)

    def process_withdrawal(self):
        """2400-PROCESS-WITHDRAWAL."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.TXN_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT TXN-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.TXN_DESC = 'WITHDRAWAL'  # MOVE 'WITHDRAWAL' TO WS-TXN-DESC
        self.WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS
        self.WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-WITHDRAWAL-COUNT
        self.update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL
        if self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE < self.WS_MIN_BALANCE_LIMIT:  # IF WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE < WS-MIN-BALANCE-LIMIT
            self.generate_low_balance_alert()  # PERFORM 2450-GENERATE-LOW-BALANCE-ALERT

    def generate_low_balance_alert(self):
        """2450-GENERATE-LOW-BALANCE-ALERT."""
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD = AlertRecord()  # INITIALIZE WS-ALERT-RECORD
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.alert_type = 'LOW-BAL'  # MOVE 'LOW-BAL' TO ALERT-TYPE
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.alert_account = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID #MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO ALERT-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.alert_balance = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE #MOVE WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE TO ALERT-BALANCE
        self.WS_ALERT_RECORD.alert_date = "20240101" #MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ALERT-DATE (placeholder - implement datetime retrieval if needed)
        #WRITE ALERT-RECORD FROM WS-ALERT-RECORD (replace with actual file writing)
        self.WS_ALERT_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ALERT-COUNT

    def process_transfer(self):
        """2500-PROCESS-TRANSFER."""
        self.validate_target_account()  # PERFORM 2510-VALIDATE-TARGET-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':  # IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
            self.debit_source()  # PERFORM 2520-DEBIT-SOURCE
            self.credit_target()  # PERFORM 2530-CREDIT-TARGET
            self.record_transfer()  # PERFORM 2540-RECORD-TRANSFER
        else:  # ELSE
            self.handle_error()  # PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR

    def validate_target_account(self):
        """2510-VALIDATE-TARGET-ACCOUNT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT #MOVE TXN-TARGET-ACCOUNT TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
        self.search_account()  # PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
        if self.WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':  # IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'N'
            self.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
            self.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'  # MOVE 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO WS-ERROR-MSG

    def debit_source(self):
        """2520-DEBIT-SOURCE."""
        self.WS_SOURCE_BALANCE -= self.TXN_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT TXN-AMOUNT FROM WS-SOURCE-BALANCE
        self.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_SOURCE_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-SOURCE-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        #REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD (replace with actual file rewriting)

    def credit_target(self):
        """2530-CREDIT-TARGET."""
        self.WS_TARGET_BALANCE += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TARGET-BALANCE
        self.ACCT_ID = self.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT  # MOVE TXN-TARGET-ACCOUNT TO ACCT-ID
        #READ MASTER-FILE INTO WS-ACCOUNT-REC (replace with actual file reading)
        self.ACCT_BALANCE = self.WS_TARGET_BALANCE  # MOVE WS-TARGET-BALANCE TO ACCT-BALANCE
        #REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD (replace with actual file rewriting)

    def record_transfer(self):
        """2540-RECORD-TRANSFER."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_TRANSFERS += self.TXN_AMOUNT  # ADD TXN-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANSFERS
        self.WS_TRANSFER_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-TRANSFER-COUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

    def process_interest(self):
        """2600-PROCESS-INTEREST."""
        self.WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT = self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE * self.WS_INTEREST_RATE / 100  # COMPUTE WS-INTEREST-AMOUNT = WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE * WS-INTEREST-RATE / 100
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT  # ADD WS-INTEREST-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.TXN_DESC = 'INTEREST'  # MOVE 'INTEREST' TO WS-TXN-DESC
        self.WS_TOTAL_INTEREST += self.WS_INTEREST_AMOUNT  # ADD WS-INTEREST-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-INTEREST
        self.WS_INTEREST_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-INTEREST-COUNT
        self.update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.write_audit_trail()  # PERFORM 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

    def handle_error(self):
        """2900-HANDLE-ERROR."""
        self.WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ERROR-COUNT
        self.WS_ERROR_RECORD = ErrorRecord()  # INITIALIZE WS-ERROR-RECORD
        self.WS_ERROR_RECORD.err_account = self.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID #MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO ERR-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_ERROR_RECORD.err_message = self.WS_ERROR_MSG #MOVE WS-ERROR-MSG TO ERR-MESSAGE
        self.WS_ERROR_RECORD.err_timestamp = "20240101" #MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ERR-TIMESTAMP (placeholder - implement datetime retrieval if needed)
        #WRITE ERROR-RECORD FROM WS-ERROR-RECORD (replace with actual file writing)

        if self.WS_ERROR_COUNT > self.WS_MAX_ERRORS:  # IF WS-ERROR-COUNT > WS-MAX-ERRORS
            self.WS_ABORT_REASON = 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED'  # MOVE 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED' TO WS-ABORT-REASON
            self.abort_process()  # PERFORM 9500-ABORT-PROCESS

    def batch_processing(self):
        """3000-BATCH-PROCESSING."""
        self.load_batch_header()  # PERFORM 3100-LOAD-BATCH-HEADER
        while self.WS_BATCH_EOF != 'Y':  # PERFORM 3200-PROCESS-BATCH-ITEMS UNTIL WS-BATCH-EOF = 'Y'
            self.process_batch_items()
        self.validate_batch_totals()  # PERFORM 3300-VALIDATE-BATCH-TOTALS
        self.commit_batch()  # PERFORM 3400-COMMIT-BATCH

    def calculate_auto_premium(self):
        """
           WHEN 6 THRU 10
              ADD 100 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           WHEN OTHER
              ADD 50 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-EVALUATE
        """
        match self.WS_DRIVER_AGE:
            case age if 6 <= age <= 10:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 100
            case _:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 50

        """
        IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 25
           MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-IF
        """
        if self.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 25:
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

        """
        IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 0
           COMPUTE WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE = 
              WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR * 200
           ADD WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-IF
        """
        if self.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 0:
            self.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE = Decimal(self.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR * 200)
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += self.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE

        """
        IF WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR > 0
           COMPUTE WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE = 
              WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR * 100
           ADD WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        END-IF
        """
        if self.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR > 0:
            self.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE = Decimal(self.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR * 100)
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += self.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE

        """
        MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
        """
        self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM

        """
        COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = 
           WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
        """
        self.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal("12.0")

    def calc_home_premium(self):
        """13230-CALC-HOME-PREMIUM.
           COMPUTE WS-BASE-PREMIUM = 
              WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT * 0.003
        """
        self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = self.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.003")

        """
           EVALUATE WS-HOME-AGE
              WHEN 0 THRU 10
                 MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 11 THRU 25
                 MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 26 THRU 50
                 MULTIPLY 1.2 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN OTHER
                 MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-EVALUATE
        """
        match self.WS_HOME_AGE:
            case age if 0 <= age <= 10:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")
            case age if 11 <= age <= 25:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
            case age if 26 <= age <= 50:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.2")
            case _:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

        """
           IF WS-FLOOD-ZONE = 'Y'
              MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-IF
        """
        if self.WS_FLOOD_ZONE == 'Y':
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

        """
           IF WS-SECURITY-SYSTEM = 'Y'
              MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-IF
        """
        if self.WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM == 'Y':
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")

        """
           COMPUTE WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT = 
              WS-DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50
        """
        self.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT = self.WS_DEDUCTIBLE / Decimal("1000.0") * 50

        """
           SUBTRACT WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT FROM WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        """
        self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM -= self.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT

        """
           IF WS-BASE-PREMIUM < 200
              MOVE 200 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-IF
        """
        if self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM < 200:
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("200.00")

        """
           MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
        """
        self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM

        """
           COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = 
              WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
        """
        self.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal("12.0")

    def calc_health_premium(self):
        """13240-CALC-HEALTH-PREMIUM.
           MOVE 300 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
        """
        self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("300.00")

        """
           EVALUATE WS-INSURED-AGE
              WHEN 0 THRU 18
                 MULTIPLY 0.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 19 THRU 30
                 MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 31 THRU 40
                 MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 41 THRU 50
                 MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 51 THRU 60
                 MULTIPLY 2.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN OTHER
                 MULTIPLY 2.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-EVALUATE
        """
        match self.WS_INSURED_AGE:
            case age if 0 <= age <= 18:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.5")
            case age if 19 <= age <= 30:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
            case age if 31 <= age <= 40:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3")
            case age if 41 <= age <= 50:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6")
            case age if 51 <= age <= 60:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.0")
            case _:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.8")

        """
           EVALUATE WS-PLAN-TYPE
              WHEN 'BRONZE'
                 MULTIPLY 0.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 'SILVER'
                 MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 'GOLD'
                 MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 'PLATINUM'
                 MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-EVALUATE
        """
        match self.WS_PLAN_TYPE:
            case 'BRONZE':
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.8")
            case 'SILVER':
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
            case 'GOLD':
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3")
            case 'PLATINUM':
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6")

        """
           IF WS-FAMILY-PLAN = 'Y'
              MULTIPLY 2.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-IF
        """
        if self.WS_FAMILY_PLAN == 'Y':
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.5")

        """
           MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM
        """
        self.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM

        """
           COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = 
              WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM * 12.
        """
        self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = self.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM * Decimal("12.0")

    def underwriting(self):
        """13300-UNDERWRITING.
           PERFORM 13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS
           PERFORM 13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY
           PERFORM 13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION
           PERFORM 13340-DETERMINE-DECISION.
        """
        self.evaluate_risk_factors()
        self.check_medical_history()
        self.verify_information()
        self.determine_decision()

    def evaluate_risk_factors(self):
        """13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-POINTS
        """
        self.WS_RISK_POINTS = 0

        """
           IF POLICY-LIFE
              IF WS-BMI > 30
                 ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              END-IF
              IF WS-SMOKER-FLAG = 'Y'
                 ADD 25 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              END-IF
              IF WS-HAZARDOUS-OCCUPATION = 'Y'
                 ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              END-IF
           END-IF
        """
        if self.POLICY_LIFE:
            if self.WS_BMI > 30:
                self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
            if self.WS_SMOKER_FLAG == 'Y':
                self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 25
            if self.WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION == 'Y':
                self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15

        """
           IF POLICY-AUTO
              IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 21
                 ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              END-IF
              IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 1
                 ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              END-IF
           END-IF.
        """
        if self.POLICY_AUTO:
            if self.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 21:
                self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
            if self.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 1:
                self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15

    def check_medical_history(self):
        """13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY.
           IF WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS > 0
              COMPUTE WS-CONDITION-POINTS = 
                 WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS * 5
              ADD WS-CONDITION-POINTS TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF
        """
        if self.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS > 0:
            self.WS_CONDITION_POINTS = self.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS * 5
            self.WS_RISK_POINTS += self.WS_CONDITION_POINTS

        """
           IF WS-RECENT-HOSPITALIZATION = 'Y'
              ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF
        """
        if self.WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y':
            self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10

        """
           IF WS-PRESCRIPTION-COUNT > 5
              ADD 5 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF.
        """
        if self.WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT > 5:
            self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 5

    def verify_information(self):
        """13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION.
           PERFORM 13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS
           PERFORM 13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS.
        """
        self.check_fraud_indicators()
        self.validate_documents()

    def check_fraud_indicators(self):
        """13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS.
           IF WS-RECENT-CLAIMS > 3
              ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-FRAUD-FLAG
           END-IF
        """
        if self.WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 3:
            self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
            self.WS_FRAUD_FLAG = 'Y'

        """
           IF WS-ADDRESS-MISMATCH = 'Y'
              ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF.
        """
        if self.WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH == 'Y':
            self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10

    def validate_documents(self):
        """13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS.
           IF WS-DOC-MISSING = 'Y'
              MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-UW-STATUS
           ELSE
              MOVE 'COMPLETE' TO WS-UW-STATUS
           END-IF.
        """
        if self.WS_DOC_MISSING == 'Y':
            self.WS_UW_STATUS = 'PENDING'
        else:
            self.WS_UW_STATUS = 'COMPLETE'

    def determine_decision(self):
        """13340-DETERMINE-DECISION.
           EVALUATE TRUE
              WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 50
                 MOVE 'DECLINE' TO WS-UW-DECISION
              WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 30
                 MOVE 'SUBSTANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
                 COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = 
                    WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 1.5
              WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 15
                 MOVE 'STANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
              WHEN OTHER
                 MOVE 'PREFERRED' TO WS-UW-DECISION
                 COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = 
                    WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 0.9
           END-EVALUATE.
        """
        match True:
            case self.WS_RISK_POINTS > 50:
                self.WS_UW_DECISION = 'DECLINE'
            case self.WS_RISK_POINTS > 30:
                self.WS_UW_DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD'
                self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")
            case self.WS_RISK_POINTS > 15:
                self.WS_UW_DECISION = 'STANDARD'
            case _:
                self.WS_UW_DECISION = 'PREFERRED'
                self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")

    def issue_policy(self):
        """13400-ISSUE-POLICY.
           IF WS-UW-DECISION NOT = 'DECLINE'
              PERFORM 13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER
              PERFORM 13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD
              PERFORM 13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES
              PERFORM 13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS
           ELSE
              PERFORM 13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER
           END-IF.
        """
        if self.WS_UW_DECISION != 'DECLINE':
            self.generate_policy_number()
            self.create_policy_record()
            self.set_beneficiaries()
            self.send_policy_docs()
        else:
            self.send_decline_letter()

    def generate_policy_number(self):
        """13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER.
           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
           MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO WS-TYPE-PART
           COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = 
              FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
           STRING WS-TYPE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE
              INTO WS-POLICY-NUMBER.
        """
        # Replace FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE and FUNCTION RANDOM with appropriate Python implementations.
        # For simplicity, using placeholders here.
        self.WS_DATE_PART = "CURRENT_DATE"  # Replace with actual date function
        self.WS_TYPE_PART = self.WS_POLICY_TYPE
        self.WS_RANDOM_PART = int(random.random() * 99999)  # Replace with actual random function

        self.WS_POLICY_NUMBER = f"{self.WS_TYPE_PART}{self.WS_DATE_PART}{self.WS_RANDOM_PART}"

    def create_policy_record(self):
        """13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD.
           INITIALIZE WS-POLICY-RECORD
           MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO POLICY-REC-NUMBER
           MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO POLICY-REC-TYPE
           MOVE WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT TO POLICY-REC-COVERAGE
           MOVE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM TO POLICY-REC-PREMIUM
           MOVE WS-EFFECTIVE-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EFF-DATE
           MOVE WS-EXPIRATION-DATE TO POLICY-REC-EXP-DATE
           MOVE 'A' TO POLICY-REC-STATUS
           WRITE POLICY-RECORD FROM WS-POLICY-RECORD.
        """
        self.WS_POLICY_RECORD = PolicyRecord()
        self.WS_POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_NUMBER = self.WS_POLICY_NUMBER
        self.WS_POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_TYPE = self.WS_POLICY_TYPE
        self.WS_POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_COVERAGE = self.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT
        self.WS_POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_PREMIUM = self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM
        self.WS_POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_EFF_DATE = self.WS_EFFECTIVE_DATE
        self.WS_POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_EXP_DATE = self.WS_EXPIRATION_DATE
        self.WS_POLICY_RECORD.POLICY_REC_STATUS = 'A'
        # WRITE POLICY-RECORD FROM WS-POLICY-RECORD would involve file I/O, omitted here

    def set_beneficiaries(self):
        """13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES.
           PERFORM VARYING WS-BENEF-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
              UNTIL WS-BENEF-IDX > 5
              IF BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX) NOT = SPACES
                 INITIALIZE WS-BENEFICIARY-REC
                 MOVE WS-POLICY-NUMBER TO BENEF-REC-POLICY
                 MOVE BENEF-NAME(WS-BENEF-IDX) 
                    TO BENEF-REC-NAME
                 MOVE BENEF-RELATION(WS-BENEF-IDX) 
                    TO BENEF-REC-RELATION
                 MOVE BENEF-PCT(WS-BENEF-IDX) TO BENEF-REC-PCT
                 WRITE BENEFICIARY-RECORD 
                    FROM WS-BENEFICIARY-REC
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM.
        """
        for self.BENEF_IDX in range(1, 6):
            if self.BENEF_NAME[self.BENEF_IDX] != "":  # Assuming empty string represents SPACES
                self.WS_BENEFICIARY_REC = BeneficiaryRecord()
                self.WS_BENEFICIARY_REC.BENEF_REC_POLICY = self.WS_POLICY_NUMBER
                self.WS_BENEFICIARY_REC.BENEF_REC_NAME = self.BENEF_NAME[self.BENEF_IDX]
                self.WS_BENEFICIARY_REC.BENEF_REC_RELATION = self.BENEF_RELATION[self.BENEF_IDX]
                self.WS_BENEFICIARY_REC.BENEF_REC_PCT = self.BENEF_PCT[self.BENEF_IDX]
                # WRITE BENEFICIARY-RECORD FROM WS-BENEFICIARY-REC would involve file I/O, omitted here

    def send_policy_docs(self):
        """13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS.
           MOVE 'POLICY-ISSUE' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
           MOVE 'MAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
           STRING 'Your policy ' DELIMITED

class CobolToPythonTranslator:

    def __init__(self):
        self.SHIP_METHOD: str = ""
        self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY: int = 0
        self.WS_PROCESS_DATE: int = 0

        self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR: str = ""

    def move(self, source: any, target: any):
        """Simulates the COBOL MOVE statement."""
        target = source
        return target

    def compute(self, expression: any) -> any:
        """Simulates the COBOL COMPUTE statement."""
        return expression

    def string(self, *args: tuple[str, str, str, str, str]) -> str:
        """Simulates the COBOL STRING statement."""
        result = ""
        for arg in args:
            result += arg
        return result

    def add(self, operand1: any, operand2: any) -> any:
        """Simulates the COBOL ADD statement."""
        return operand1 + operand2

    def subtract(self, operand1: any, operand2: any) -> any:
        """Simulates the COBOL SUBTRACT statement."""
        return operand1 - operand2

    def initialize(self, variable: any):
        """Simulates the COBOL INITIALIZE statement."""
        if isinstance(variable, str):
            return ""
        elif isinstance(variable, int):
            return 0
        elif isinstance(variable, Decimal):
            return Decimal("0.00")
        elif isinstance(variable, list):
            return []
        else:
            # Create a new instance of the dataclass
            return type(variable)()

    def perform_15000_send_notification(self):
        """ Placeholder for 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION."""
        pass

    def perform_5000_search_account(self):
        """ Placeholder for 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT."""
        pass

        pass

    def perform_26620_email_statement(self):
        """ Placeholder for 26620-EMAIL-STATEMENT."""
        pass

    def process_shipment(self):
        """
        COBOL Equivalent:
            IF WS-EXPEDITED-SHIPPING = 'Y'
              MOVE 'EXPRESS' TO SHIP-METHOD
              COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY = 
                 FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 2
           ELSE
              MOVE 'STANDARD' TO SHIP-METHOD
              COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY = 
                 FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 7
           END-IF
           WRITE SHIPMENT-RECORD FROM WS-SHIPMENT-RECORD.
        """
        if self.WS_EXPEDITED_SHIPPING == 'Y':
            self.SHIP_METHOD = 'EXPRESS'
            self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = self.compute(self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 2)
        else:
            self.SHIP_METHOD = 'STANDARD'
            self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = self.compute(self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 7)
        # Assuming write_shipment_record is a function to handle the WRITE
        self.write_shipment_record(self.WS_SHIPMENT_RECORD)

    def write_shipment_record(self, record: WsShipmentRecord):
        """ Placeholder for the WRITE statement """
        pass
    
    def integer_of_date(self, date: int) -> int:
        """ Placeholder for INTEGER-OF-DATE function """
        return date

    def card_blocking(self):
        """
        23500-CARD-BLOCKING.
           MOVE 'B' TO CARD-STATUS
           MOVE WS-BLOCK-REASON TO CARD-BLOCK-REASON
           MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO CARD-BLOCK-DATE
           REWRITE CARD-RECORD FROM WS-CARD-RECORD
           MOVE 'CARD-BLOCKED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
           MOVE 'SMS' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
           STRING 'Your card has been blocked: ' DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-BLOCK-REASON DELIMITED SIZE
              INTO WS-NOTIF-BODY
           PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
        """
        self.CARD_STATUS = 'B'
        self.CARD_BLOCK_REASON = self.WS_BLOCK_REASON
        self.CARD_BLOCK_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.rewrite_card_record(self.WS_CARD_RECORD)
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'CARD-BLOCKED'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS'
        self.WS_NOTIF_BODY = self.string('Your card has been blocked: ', self.WS_BLOCK_REASON)
        self.perform_15000_send_notification()

    def rewrite_card_record(self, record: WsCardRecord):
        """ Placeholder for the REWRITE statement """
        pass

    def wire_transfer(self):
        """
        24000-WIRE-TRANSFER.
           PERFORM 24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST
           IF WS-WIRE-VALID = 'Y'
              PERFORM 24200-OFAC-SCREENING
              IF WS-OFAC-CLEAR = 'Y'
                 PERFORM 24300-PROCESS-WIRE
                 PERFORM 24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION
              ELSE
                 PERFORM 24500-REJECT-WIRE
              END-IF
           END-IF.
        """
        self.validate_wire_request()
        if self.WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
            self.ofac_screening()
            if self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
                self.process_wire()
                self.send_confirmation()
            else:
                self.reject_wire()

    def validate_wire_request(self):
        """
        24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST.
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
           IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT <= 0
              MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
              MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
           END-IF
           IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT > WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
              MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
              MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
           END-IF
           IF WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT = SPACES
              MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
              MOVE 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
           END-IF
           IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT > 10000
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CTR-REQUIRED
           END-IF.
        """
        self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
        if self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == self.SPACES:
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
            self.WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

    def ofac_screening(self):
        """
        24200-OFAC-SCREENING.
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
           MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO OFAC-SEARCH-NAME
           CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
           IF OFAC-MATCH-FOUND = 'Y'
              IF OFAC-MATCH-SCORE >= 85
                 MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                 MOVE 'OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
              END-IF
           END-IF
           MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK TO OFAC-SEARCH-BANK
           CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
           IF OFAC-MATCH-FOUND = 'Y'
              IF OFAC-MATCH-SCORE >= 85
                 MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                 MOVE 'BANK OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
              END-IF
           END-IF.
        """
        self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
        self.OFAC_SEARCH_NAME = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
        self.ofacsrch(self.OFAC_REQUEST, self.OFAC_RESPONSE)
        if self.OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
            if self.OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
        self.OFAC_SEARCH_BANK = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
        self.ofacsrch(self.OFAC_REQUEST, self.OFAC_RESPONSE)
        if self.OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
            if self.OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

    def ofacsrch(self, ofac_request: OfacRequest, ofac_response: OfacResponse):
        """ Placeholder for the CALL statement OFACSRCH """
        pass
    
    def process_wire(self):
        """
        24300-PROCESS-WIRE.
           PERFORM 24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR
           PERFORM 24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE
           PERFORM 24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE
           PERFORM 24340-RECORD-WIRE.
        """
        self.debit_originator()
        self.create_wire_message()
        self.transmit_wire()
        self.record_wire()

    def debit_originator(self):
        """
        24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR.
           SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
           SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-FEE FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
           PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT.
        """
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE = self.subtract(self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT)
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE = self.subtract(self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, self.WS_WIRE_FEE)
        self.perform_2350_update_account()

    def create_wire_message(self):
        """
        24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE.
           INITIALIZE WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE
           MOVE 'MT103' TO SWIFT-MSG-TYPE
           MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO SWIFT-TXN-REF
           MOVE WS-WIRE-DATE TO SWIFT-VALUE-DATE
           MOVE WS-WIRE-CURRENCY TO SWIFT-CURRENCY
           MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO SWIFT-AMOUNT
           MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-NAME TO SWIFT-ORDERING-CUST
           MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-ORDERING-ACCT
           MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO SWIFT-BENEF-CUST
           MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-BENEF-ACCT
           MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK-BIC TO SWIFT-BENEF-BANK
           MOVE WS-PURPOSE TO SWIFT-REMIT-INFO.
        """
        self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE = self.initialize(self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE)
        self.SWIFT_MSG_TYPE = 'MT103'
        self.SWIFT_TXN_REF = self.WS_WIRE_REF
        self.SWIFT_VALUE_DATE = self.WS_WIRE_DATE
        self.SWIFT_CURRENCY = self.WS_WIRE_CURRENCY
        self.SWIFT_AMOUNT = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
        self.SWIFT_ORDERING_CUST = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME
        self.SWIFT_ORDERING_ACCT = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
        self.SWIFT_BENEF_CUST = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
        self.SWIFT_BENEF_ACCT = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
        self.SWIFT_BENEF_BANK = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC
        self.SWIFT_REMIT_INFO = self.WS_PURPOSE

    def transmit_wire(self):
        """
        24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE.
           CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE 
              WS-SWIFT-RESPONSE
           IF SWIFT-STATUS = 'ACK'
              MOVE 'SENT' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
           ELSE
              MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
              PERFORM 24350-REVERSE-DEBIT
           END-IF.
        """
        self.swiftsend(self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, self.WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE)
        if self.SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
        else:
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
            self.reverse_debit()

    def swiftsend(self, ws_swift_message: WsSwiftMessage, ws_swift_response: WsSwiftResponse):
        """ Placeholder for the CALL statement SWIFTSEND """
        pass

    def record_wire(self):
        """
        24340-RECORD-WIRE.
           INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-RECORD
           MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO WIRE-REF
           MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WIRE-AMOUNT
           MOVE WS-WIRE-STATUS TO WIRE-STATUS
           MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-FROM-ACCT
           MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-TO-ACCT
           MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO WIRE-DATE
           WRITE WIRE-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-RECORD.
        """
        self.WS_WIRE_RECORD = self.initialize(self.WS_WIRE_RECORD)
        self.WIRE_REF = self.WS_WIRE_REF
        self.WIRE_AMOUNT = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
        self.WIRE_STATUS = self.WS_WIRE_STATUS
        self.WIRE_FROM_ACCT = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
        self.WIRE_TO_ACCT = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
        self.WIRE_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.write_wire_record(self.WS_WIRE_RECORD)

    def write_wire_record(self, record: WsWireRecord):
        """ Placeholder for the WRITE statement for WIRE-RECORD """
        pass

    def reverse_debit(self):
        """
        24350-REVERSE-DEBIT.
           ADD WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
           ADD WS-WIRE-FEE TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
           PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT.
        """
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE = self.add(self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT)
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE = self.add(self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, self.WS_WIRE_FEE)
        self.perform_2350_update_account()

    def send_confirmation(self):
        """
        24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION.
           MOVE 'WIRE-CONFIRM' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
           MOVE 'EMAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
           STRING 'Wire transfer ' DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-WIRE-REF DELIMITED SIZE
                  ' completed' DELIMITED SIZE
              INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
           PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION.
        """
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-CONFIRM'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = self.string('Wire transfer ', self.WS_WIRE_REF, ' completed')
        self.perform_15000_send_notification()

    def reject_wire(self):
        """
        24500-REJECT-WIRE.
           MOVE 'REJECTED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
           INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
           MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO REJECT-WIRE-REF
           MOVE WS-WIRE-REJECT TO REJECT-REASON
           MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO REJECT-DATE
           WRITE WIRE-REJECT-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
           MOVE 'WIRE-REJECTED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
           PERFORM 15

class CobolProgram:

    def __init__(self):
        self.ACCT_ID: str = ""
        self.WS_STOP_VALID: str = ""
        self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER: int = 0
        self.WS_STOP_REJECT: str = ""
        self.WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED: str = ""
