    def validate_stop_request(self) -> None:
        """29100-VALIDATE-STOP-REQUEST."""
        self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'
        if self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER == 0:
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
        if self.WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y':
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

    def create_stop_order(self) -> None:
        """29200-CREATE-STOP-ORDER."""
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD = WsStopRecord()
        self.STOP_ACCOUNT = self.ACCT_ID
        self.STOP_CHECK_NUMBER = str(self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER)
        self.STOP_AMOUNT = self.WS_CHECK_AMOUNT
        self.STOP_PAYEE = self.WS_PAYEE_NAME
        self.STOP_EFFECTIVE_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.STOP_EXPIRY_DATE = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 180
        self.STOP_STATUS = 'A'
        self.write_stop_record(self.WS_STOP_RECORD) # Assuming you have a method to write the record

    def apply_stop_fee(self) -> None:
        """29300-APPLY-STOP-FEE."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE
        self.update_account()  # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'STOP-PAYMENT'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Stop payment placed on check # {self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER}'
        self.send_notification() # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

    def safe_deposit_box(self) -> None:
        """30000-SAFE-DEPOSIT-BOX."""
        self.box_rental() # PERFORM 30100-BOX-RENTAL
        self.box_access() # PERFORM 30200-BOX-ACCESS
        self.box_drilling() # PERFORM 30300-BOX-DRILLING
        self.box_billing() # PERFORM 30400-BOX-BILLING

    def box_rental(self) -> None:
        """30100-BOX-RENTAL."""
        if self.WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.check_availability() # PERFORM 30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY
            if self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                self.assign_box() # PERFORM 30120-ASSIGN-BOX
                self.create_rental_agreement() # PERFORM 30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT

    def check_availability(self) -> None:
        """30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY."""
        self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N'
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
            if self.BOX_STATUS.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == 'A':
                if self.BOX_SIZE.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE:
                    self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
                    self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX = self.WS_BOX_IDX
                    break
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1

    def assign_box(self) -> None:
        """30120-ASSIGN-BOX."""
        self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX] = 'R'
        self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX] = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.BOX_RENTAL_DATE[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX] = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE

    def create_rental_agreement(self) -> None:
        """30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT."""
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = WsRentalAgreement()
        self.RENTAL_BOX_NUMBER = str(self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX)
        self.RENTAL_CUSTOMER = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.RENTAL_START_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.RENTAL_ANNUAL_FEE = self.WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE.get(self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, Decimal("0.00"))
        self.write_rental_record(self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT) # Assuming a method exists to write rental record

    def box_access(self) -> None:
        """30200-BOX-ACCESS."""
        if self.WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.verify_renter() # PERFORM 30210-VERIFY-RENTER
            if self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.log_access() # PERFORM 30220-LOG-ACCESS
                self.escort_to_vault() # PERFORM 30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT

    def verify_renter(self) -> None:
        """30210-VERIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N'
        if self.BOX_RENTER.get(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER) == self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID:
            if self.WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                if self.WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                    self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'

    def log_access(self) -> None:
        """30220-LOG-ACCESS."""
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG = WsAccessLog()
        self.ACCESS_BOX_NUMBER = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.ACCESS_CUSTOMER = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.ACCESS_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.ACCESS_TIME = self.current_time() # FUNCTION CURRENT-TIME
        self.ACCESS_TYPE = 'ENTRY'
        self.write_access_log_record(self.WS_ACCESS_LOG) # Assuming a method exists to write access log

    def escort_to_vault(self) -> None:
        """30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT."""
        self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
        self.display_message(self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG) # DISPLAY WS-DISPLAY-MSG

    def box_drilling(self) -> None:
        """30300-BOX-DRILLING."""
        if self.WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.validate_drilling_auth() # PERFORM 30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH
            if self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y':
                self.schedule_drilling() # PERFORM 30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING
                self.notify_renter() # PERFORM 30330-NOTIFY-RENTER

    def validate_drilling_auth(self) -> None:
        """30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N'
        if self.WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12:
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y':
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y':
            if self.WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

    def schedule_drilling(self) -> None:
        """30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD = WsDrillingRecord()
        self.DRILL_BOX_NUMBER = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.DRILL_REASON = self.WS_DRILLING_REASON
        self.DRILL_SCHEDULED_DATE = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 30
        self.write_drilling_record(self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD) # Assuming method to write record exists

    def notify_renter(self) -> None:
        """30330-NOTIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'BOX-DRILLING'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
        self.send_notification() # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

    def box_billing(self) -> None:
        """30400-BOX-BILLING."""
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
            if self.BOX_STATUS.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == 'R':
                if self.BOX_RENEWAL_DUE.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == 'Y':
                    self.charge_annual_fee() # PERFORM 30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1

    def charge_annual_fee(self) -> None:
        """30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE."""
        self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID = self.BOX_RENTER.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX)
        self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT = self.BOX_ANNUAL_FEE.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX, Decimal("0.00"))
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT
        self.update_account() # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX] = self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX, 0) + 10000

    def merchant_services(self) -> None:
        """31000-MERCHANT-SERVICES."""
        self.process_authorization() # PERFORM 31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION
        self.capture_transaction() # PERFORM 31200-CAPTURE-TRANSACTION
        self.process_settlement() # PERFORM 31300-PROCESS-SETTLEMENT
        self.handle_chargeback() # PERFORM 31400-HANDLE-CHARGEBACK

    def process_authorization(self) -> None:
        """31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.validate_card() # PERFORM 31110-VALIDATE-CARD
        if self.WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_fraud_score() # PERFORM 31120-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE
            if self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y':
                self.check_available_credit() # PERFORM 31130-CHECK-AVAILABLE-CREDIT
                if self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                    self.approve_auth() # PERFORM 31140-APPROVE-AUTH
                else:
                    self.decline_auth() # PERFORM 31150-DECLINE-AUTH
            else:
                self.decline_auth() # PERFORM 31150-DECLINE-AUTH
        else:
            self.decline_auth() # PERFORM 31150-DECLINE-AUTH

    def validate_card(self) -> None:
        """31110-VALIDATE-CARD."""
        self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'
        self.check_luhn() # PERFORM 31115-CHECK-LUHN
        if self.WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_expiry() # PERFORM 31116-CHECK-EXPIRY
            if self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y':
                self.check_cvv() # PERFORM 31117-CHECK-CVV
                if self.WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y':
                    self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'

    def check_luhn(self) -> None:
        """31115-CHECK-LUHN."""
        self.WS_LUHN_SUM = 0
        self.WS_LUHN_IDX = 16
        while self.WS_LUHN_IDX >= 1:
            self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[self.WS_LUHN_IDX - 1])
            if (17 - self.WS_LUHN_IDX) % 2 == 0:
                self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2
                if self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9:
                    self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9
            self.WS_LUHN_SUM += self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT
            self.WS_LUHN_IDX -= 1

        if self.WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N'

    def check_expiry(self) -> None:
        """31116-CHECK-EXPIRY."""
        if self.WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= self.WS_PROCESS_DATE:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N'

    def check_cvv(self) -> None:
        """31117-CHECK-CVV."""
        cvv_result = self.cvvverify(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, self.WS_AUTH_CVV) # CALL 'CVVVERIFY'
        if cvv_result == 'M':
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'N'

    def check_fraud_score(self) -> None:
        """31120-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE."""
        fraud_response = self.fraudcheck(self.WS_AUTH_REQUEST)  # CALL 'FRAUDCHECK'
        if self.FRAUD_SCORE < 70:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = self.FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE

    def check_available_credit(self) -> None:
        """31130-CHECK-AVAILABLE-CREDIT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        card_account_rec = self.read_card_account_file(self.WS_SEARCH_KEY) #READ CARD-ACCOUNT-FILE
        if card_account_rec and self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51'

    def approve_auth(self) -> None:
        """31140-APPROVE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = '00'
        self.generate_auth_code() # PERFORM 31145-GENERATE-AUTH-CODE
        self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT -= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.record_authorization() # PERFORM 31146-RECORD-AUTHORIZATION

    def generate_auth_code(self) -> None:
        """31145-GENERATE-AUTH-CODE."""

class MegaEnterprise:

    def __init__(self):
        self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT = WsTreasuryManagement()
        self.WS_LIQUIDITY_MANAGEMENT = WsLiquidityManagement()
        self.WS_CAPITAL_MANAGEMENT = WsCapitalManagement()
        self.WS_ASSET_LIABILITY_MGMT = WsAssetLiabilityMgmt()
        self.WS_STRESS_TESTING = WsStressTesting()
        self.WS_MODEL_VALIDATION = WsModelValidation()
        self.WS_COLLATERAL_MANAGEMENT = WsCollateralManagement()
        self.WS_DERIVATIVE_POSITION = WsDerivativePosition()
        self.WS_HEDGE_ACCOUNTING = WsHedgeAccounting()
        self.WS_SECURITIZATION = WsSecuritization()
        self.WS_REGULATORY_REPORTING = WsRegulatoryReporting()
        self.WS_GENERAL_LEDGER = WsGeneralLedger()
        self.WS_JOURNAL_ENTRY = WsJournalEntry()
        self.WS_RECONCILIATION = WsReconciliation()
        self.WS_AUDIT_TRAIL_EXT = WsAuditTrailExt()
        self.WS_FILE_RESULT = ""

    def error_handling(self):
        """99900-ERROR-HANDLING."""
        self.format_error()
        self.display_error()
        self.write_error_log()

    def format_error(self):
        """99910-FORMAT-ERROR."""
        self.WS_FORMATTED_ERROR = f"ERROR: {self.WS_ERROR_CODE} - {self.WS_ERROR_MSG}"

    def display_error(self):
        """99920-DISPLAY-ERROR."""
        print(self.WS_FORMATTED_ERROR)

    def write_error_log(self):
        """99930-WRITE-ERROR-LOG."""
        self.WS_ERROR_LOG_REC = WsErrorLogRec()
        self.WS_ERROR_LOG_REC.err_log_code = self.WS_ERROR_CODE
        self.WS_ERROR_LOG_REC.err_log_msg = self.WS_ERROR_MSG
        self.WS_ERROR_LOG_REC.err_log_timestamp = current_date()
        self.WS_ERROR_LOG_REC.err_log_program = self.WS_PROGRAM_NAME
        self.WS_ERROR_LOG_REC.err_log_paragraph = self.WS_PARAGRAPH_NAME
        # Assuming the write operation is handled elsewhere.
        pass

    def treasury_management(self):
        """32000-TREASURY-MANAGEMENT."""
        self.calculate_cash_position()
        self.project_cash_flows()
        self.manage_reserves()
        self.manage_investments()
        self.manage_borrowings()

    def calculate_cash_position(self):
        """32100-CALCULATE-CASH-POSITION."""
        self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_cash_position = Decimal("0.00")
        self.sum_vault_cash()
        self.sum_fed_account()
        self.sum_correspondent_balances()

    def sum_vault_cash(self):
        """32110-SUM-VAULT-CASH."""
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            # Simulate reading from a file
            # READ VAULT-CASH-FILE INTO WS-VAULT-REC
            # AT END
            #    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            # NOT AT END
            if True: # Replace with actual read condition
                self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_cash_position += self.VAULT_BALANCE
            else:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def sum_fed_account(self):
        """32120-SUM-FED-ACCOUNT."""
        # Simulate reading from a file
        # READ FED-ACCOUNT-FILE INTO WS-FED-BALANCE
        #ADD WS-FED-BALANCE TO WS-CASH-POSITION
        self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_cash_position += self.WS_FED_BALANCE

    def sum_correspondent_balances(self):
        """32130-SUM-CORRESPONDENT-BALANCES."""
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            # Simulate reading from a file
            # READ CORRESPONDENT-FILE INTO WS-CORR-REC
            # AT END
            #    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            # NOT AT END
            if True: # Replace with actual read condition
                self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_cash_position += self.CORR_BALANCE
            else:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def project_cash_flows(self):
        """32200-PROJECT-CASH-FLOWS."""
        self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_inflows = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_outflows = Decimal("0.00")
        self.project_loan_payments()
        self.project_deposit_flows()
        self.project_investment_maturities()
        self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_net_position = (
            self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_cash_position +
            self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_inflows -
            self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_outflows
        )

    def project_loan_payments(self):
        """32210-PROJECT-LOAN-PAYMENTS."""
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            # Simulate reading from a file
            # READ LOAN-SCHEDULE-FILE INTO WS-LOAN-PMT-REC
            # AT END
            #    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            # NOT AT END
            if True: # Replace with actual read condition
                if self.LOAN_PMT_DATE <= self.WS_PROJECTION_DATE:
                    self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_inflows += self.LOAN_PMT_AMOUNT
            else:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def project_deposit_flows(self):
        """32220-PROJECT-DEPOSIT-FLOWS."""
        self.WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS = self.WS_AVG_DAILY_DEPOSITS * self.WS_PROJECTION_DAYS
        self.WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS = self.WS_AVG_DAILY_WITHDRAWALS * self.WS_PROJECTION_DAYS
        self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_inflows += self.WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS
        self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_outflows += self.WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS

    def project_investment_maturities(self):
        """32230-PROJECT-INVESTMENT-MATURITIES."""
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            # Simulate reading from a file
            # READ INVESTMENT-FILE INTO WS-INV-REC
            # AT END
            #    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            # NOT AT END
            if True: # Replace with actual read condition
                if self.INV_MATURITY_DATE <= self.WS_PROJECTION_DATE:
                    self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_inflows += self.INV_PAR_VALUE
            else:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def manage_reserves(self):
        """32300-MANAGE-RESERVES."""
        self.calculate_reserve_requirement()
        self.check_reserve_position()
        if self.WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY == 'Y':
            self.cover_reserve_shortfall()
        else:
            self.invest_excess_reserves()

    def calculate_reserve_requirement(self):
        """32310-CALCULATE-RESERVE-REQUIREMENT."""
        self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_reserve_requirement = (
            self.WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS * self.WS_RESERVE_RATIO
        )

    def check_reserve_position(self):
        """32320-CHECK-RESERVE-POSITION."""
        self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_excess_reserves = (
            self.WS_FED_BALANCE - self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_reserve_requirement
        )
        if self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_excess_reserves < 0:
            self.WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY = 'N'

    def cover_reserve_shortfall(self):
        """32330-COVER-RESERVE-SHORTFALL."""
        self.WS_SHORTFALL_AMOUNT = Decimal("0") - self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_excess_reserves
        self.borrow_fed_funds()

    def borrow_fed_funds(self):
        """32335-BORROW-FED-FUNDS."""
        #INITIALIZE WS-FED-FUNDS-TRANSACTION
        self.FF_TRANS_TYPE = 'BORROW'
        self.FF_AMOUNT = self.WS_SHORTFALL_AMOUNT
        self.FF_RATE = self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_fed_funds_rate
        self.FF_SETTLE_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.FF_MATURITY_DATE = integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 1
        #WRITE FED-FUNDS-RECORD FROM WS-FED-FUNDS-TRANSACTION.
        pass

    def invest_excess_reserves(self):
        """32340-INVEST-EXCESS-RESERVES."""
        if self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_excess_reserves > self.WS_MIN_INVEST_AMOUNT:
            self.sell_fed_funds()

    def sell_fed_funds(self):
        """32345-SELL-FED-FUNDS."""
        #INITIALIZE WS-FED-FUNDS-TRANSACTION
        self.FF_TRANS_TYPE = 'SELL'
        self.FF_AMOUNT = self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_excess_reserves
        self.FF_RATE = self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_fed_funds_rate
        self.FF_SETTLE_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.FF_MATURITY_DATE = integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 1
        #WRITE FED-FUNDS-RECORD FROM WS-FED-FUNDS-TRANSACTION.
        pass

    def manage_investments(self):
        """32400-MANAGE-INVESTMENTS."""
        self.review_investment_portfolio()
        self.execute_investment_strategy()
        self.mark_to_market()

    def review_investment_portfolio(self):
        """32410-REVIEW-INVESTMENT-PORTFOLIO."""
        self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_investment_pool = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_AVG_YIELD = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_AVG_DURATION = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_TOTAL_YIELD = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_TOTAL_DURATION = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_INV_COUNT = 0
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            # Simulate reading from a file
            # READ INVESTMENT-FILE INTO WS-INV-REC
            # AT END
            #    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            # NOT AT END
            if True: # Replace with actual read condition
                self.WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_investment_pool += self.INV_MARKET_VALUE
                self.WS_TOTAL_YIELD += self.INV_YIELD
                self.WS_TOTAL_DURATION += self.INV_DURATION
                self.WS_INV_COUNT += 1
            else:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

        if self.WS_INV_COUNT > 0:
            self.WS_AVG_YIELD = self.WS_TOTAL_YIELD / self.WS_INV_COUNT
            self.WS_AVG_DURATION = self.WS_TOTAL_DURATION / self.WS_INV_COUNT

        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def execute_investment_strategy(self):
        """32420-EXECUTE-INVESTMENT-STRATEGY."""
        match self.WS_RATE_OUTLOOK:
            case 'RISING':
                self.shorten_duration()
            case 'FALLING':
                self.extend_duration()
            case 'STABLE':
                self.maintain_position()

    def

    data.cust_status = 'I'

    """B110-CAPITAL-RATIOS.

        WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS * 0.08."""

    c200_case_management()
    c300_sar_filing(data)
    c400_watchlist_screening()
    c500_beneficial_ownership()

def c100_transaction_monitoring(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """C100-TRANSACTION-MONITORING.
    DISPLAY "MONITORING TRANSACTIONS..."
    SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        READ TRANSACTION-LOG NEXT
            AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION
                PERFORM C120-BEHAVIOR-ANALYSIS
                PERFORM C130-NETWORK-ANALYSIS
        END-READ
    END-PERFORM."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    data.ws_not_eof = True
    while not data.ws_eof:
        try:
            tran = transaction_log.pop(0)
            data.tran_amount = tran
            c110_rule_based_detection(data)
            c120_behavior_analysis()
            c130_network_analysis()
        except IndexError:
            data.ws_eof = True

def c110_rule_based_detection(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION.
    IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 10000
        PERFORM C111-FLAG-CTR
    END-IF
    IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 5000 AND TRAN-AMOUNT < 10000
        PERFORM C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING
    END-IF."""
    if data.tran_amount >= Decimal("10000"):
        c111_flag_ctr(data)
    if Decimal("5000") <= data.tran_amount < Decimal("10000"):
        c112_check_structuring(data)

def c111_flag_ctr(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """C111-FLAG-CTR.
    ADD 1 TO WS-PROCESS-COUNT."""
    data.ws_process_count += 1

def c112_check_structuring(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING.
    ADD 1 TO WS-ERROR-COUNT."""
    data.ws_error_count += 1

def d100_machine_learning(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """D100-MACHINE-LEARNING.
    DISPLAY "RUNNING MACHINE LEARNING MODELS..."
    PERFORM D110-CLASSIFICATION
    PERFORM D120-REGRESSION
    PERFORM D130-CLUSTERING."""
    print("RUNNING MACHINE LEARNING MODELS...")
    d110_classification(data)
    d120_regression(data)
    d130_clustering()

def d110_classification(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """D110-CLASSIFICATION.
    IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 750
        MOVE 'A' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 650
        MOVE 'B' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 550
        MOVE 'C' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    ELSE
        MOVE 'D' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    END-IF
    END-IF
    END-IF."""
    if data.cust_credit_score > 750:
        data.cust_risk_rating = 'A'
    elif data.cust_credit_score > 650:
        data.cust_risk_rating = 'B'
    elif data.cust_credit_score > 550:
        data.cust_risk_rating = 'C'
    else:
        data.cust_risk_rating = 'D'

def d120_regression(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """D120-REGRESSION.
    COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT = 
        (CUST-CREDIT-SCORE * 10) +
        (CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE / 1000) -
        (CUST-TOTAL-LOANS / 2000)."""
    data.ws_calc_result = Decimal(data.cust_credit_score * 10) + (data.cust_total_balance / Decimal("1000")) - (data.cust_total_loans / Decimal("2000"))

def f220_contract_execution(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """F220-CONTRACT-EXECUTION.
    IF LOAN-CURRENT-BALANCE = 0

def determine_interest_rate(interest_method: str) -> Decimal:
    """
    000090 EVALUATE WS-INTEREST-METHOD
    """
    match interest_method:
        case 'S':
            """
            000100 WHEN 'S'
            """
            return Decimal('1.5')
        case 'C':
            """
            000110 WHEN 'C'
            """
            return Decimal('1.75')
        case _:
            """
            000120 WHEN OTHER
                 MOVE 2.0 TO WS-INTEREST-RATE
            """
            return Decimal('2.0')

def set_interest_rate(interest_method: str) -> Decimal:
    """
    000150 EVALUATE WS-CUSTOMER-TIER
    """
    match interest_method:
        case 'GOLD':
            """
            000160 WHEN 'GOLD'
            """
            return Decimal('2.25')
        case 'PLATINUM':
            """
            000170 WHEN 'PLATINUM'
            """
            return Decimal('2.35')
        case _:
            """
            000180 WHEN OTHER
                 MOVE 2.5 TO WS-INTEREST-RATE
            """
            return Decimal('2.5')

def calculate_simple_interest(account_balance: Decimal, interest_rate: Decimal, days_in_period: int) -> Decimal:
    """7200-CALCULATE-SIMPLE-INTEREST."""
    return account_balance * interest_rate * days_in_period / Decimal('36500')

def calculate_compound_interest(account_balance: Decimal, interest_rate: Decimal, days_in_period: int) -> Decimal:
    """7300-CALCULATE-COMPOUND-INTEREST."""
    compound_factor = (1 + interest_rate / Decimal('36500')) ** days_in_period
    return account_balance * (compound_factor - 1)

def apply_interest(interest_method: str, simple_interest: Decimal, compound_interest: Decimal, account_balance: Decimal, update_account: callable) -> Decimal:
    """7400-APPLY-INTEREST."""
    if interest_method == 'S':
        account_balance += simple_interest
    else:
        account_balance += compound_interest
    update_account()
    return account_balance

def fee_processing(calculate_monthly_fee: callable, calculate_transaction_fees: callable, apply_fee_waivers: callable, deduct_fees: callable) -> None:
    """8000-FEE-PROCESSING."""
    calculate_monthly_fee()
    calculate_transaction_fees()
    apply_fee_waivers()
    deduct_fees()

def calculate_monthly_fee(account_type: str) -> Decimal:
    """8100-CALCULATE-MONTHLY-FEE."""
    match account_type:
        case 'CHK':
            """8100-CALCULATE-MONTHLY-FEE."""
            return Decimal('12.00')
        case 'SAV':
            """8100-CALCULATE-MONTHLY-FEE."""
            return Decimal('5.00')
        case 'PRM':
            """8100-CALCULATE-MONTHLY-FEE."""
            return Decimal('25.00')
        case _:
            """8100-CALCULATE-MONTHLY-FEE."""
            return Decimal('0.00')

def calculate_transaction_fees(trans_count: int, free_trans_limit: int, per_trans_fee: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """8200-CALCULATE-TRANSACTION-FEES."""
    if trans_count > free_trans_limit:
        excess_trans = trans_count - free_trans_limit
        trans_fee = excess_trans * per_trans_fee
    else:
        trans_fee = Decimal('0')
    return trans_fee

def apply_fee_waivers(account_balance: Decimal, min_balance_waiver: Decimal, customer_tier: str, trans_fee: Decimal, monthly_fee: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """8300-APPLY-FEE-WAIVERS."""
    if account_balance >= min_balance_waiver:
        monthly_fee = Decimal('0')
    if customer_tier == 'GOLD' or customer_tier == 'PLATINUM':
        trans_fee *= Decimal('0.5')
    return trans_fee, monthly_fee

def deduct_fees(monthly_fee: Decimal, trans_fee: Decimal, account_balance: Decimal, update_account: callable, record_fee_transaction: callable) -> Decimal:
    """8400-DEDUCT-FEES."""
    total_fees = monthly_fee + trans_fee
    account_balance -= total_fees
    update_account()
    record_fee_transaction(total_fees)
    return account_balance

def record_fee_transaction(txn_account_id: str, total_fees: Decimal, fee_record: callable, current_date: callable) -> None:
    """8450-RECORD-FEE-TRANSACTION."""
    fee_record_obj = fee_record()
    fee_record_obj.fee_account = txn_account_id
    fee_record_obj.fee_amount = total_fees
    fee_record_obj.fee_description = 'MONTHLY FEE'
    fee_record_obj.fee_date = current_date()
    # WRITE FEE-RECORD FROM WS-FEE-RECORD. (Assume write operation handled elsewhere)
    return fee_record_obj

def finalization(write_control_totals: callable, close_files: callable, display_summary: callable) -> None:
    """9000-FINALIZATION."""
    write_control_totals()
    close_files()
    display_summary()

def write_control_totals(trans_count: int, total_deposits: Decimal, total_withdrawals: Decimal, error_count: int, control_record: callable, current_date: callable) -> None:
    """9100-WRITE-CONTROL-TOTALS."""
    control_record_obj = control_record()
    control_record_obj.ctl_trans_count = trans_count
    control_record_obj.ctl_deposits = total_deposits
    control_record_obj.ctl_withdrawals = total_withdrawals
    control_record_obj.ctl_error_count = error_count
    control_record_obj.ctl_run_date = current_date()
    # WRITE CONTROL-RECORD FROM WS-CONTROL-RECORD (Assume file write operation handled elsewhere)
    return control_record_obj

def close_files(customer_file_close: callable, account_file_close: callable, transaction_file_close: callable, report_file_close: callable, error_file_close: callable, master_file_close: callable) -> None:
    """9200-CLOSE-FILES."""
    customer_file_close()
    account_file_close()
    transaction_file_close()
    report_file_close()
    error_file_close()
    master_file_close()

def display_summary(ws_trans_count: int, ws_deposit_count: int, ws_withdrawal_count: int, ws_transfer_count: int, ws_error_count: int, ws_total_deposits: Decimal, ws_total_withdrawals: Decimal, ws_net_change: Decimal) -> None:
    """9300-DISPLAY-SUMMARY."""
    print('==========================================')
    print('MEGA-ENTERPRISE PROCESSING COMPLETE')
    print('==========================================')
    print(f'TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: {ws_trans_count}')
    print(f'DEPOSITS:              {ws_deposit_count}')
    print(f'WITHDRAWALS:           {ws_withdrawal_count}')
    print(f'TRANSFERS:             {ws_transfer_count}')
    print(f'ERRORS:                {ws_error_count}')
    print(f'TOTAL DEPOSITS:   ${ws_total_deposits}')
    print(f'TOTAL WITHDRAWALS:$ {ws_total_withdrawals}')
    print(f'NET CHANGE:       $ {ws_net_change}')
    print('==========================================')

def abort_process(abort_reason: str, close_files: callable, current_date: callable) -> None:
    """9500-ABORT-PROCESS."""
    print(f'CRITICAL ERROR: {abort_reason}')
    print(f'PROCESSING ABORTED AT {current_date()}')
    close_files()
    raise SystemExit(8)

def loan_processing(validate_loan_application: callable, calculate_credit_score: callable, assess_risk: callable, determine_approval: callable, generate_loan_terms: callable, create_amortization: callable, finalize_loan: callable, process_decline: callable, ws_valid_flag: str, ws_approval_status: str) -> None:
    """10000-LOAN-PROCESSING."""
    validate_loan_application()
    if ws_valid_flag == 'Y':
        calculate_credit_score()
        assess_risk()
        determine_approval()
        if ws_approval_status == 'A':
            generate_loan_terms()
            create_amortization()
            finalize_loan()
        else:
            process_decline()

def validate_loan_application(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_valid_flag: str, ws_error_msg: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """10100-VALIDATE-LOAN-APPLICATION."""
    ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_loan_amount < 1000:
        ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        ws_error_msg = 'MINIMUM LOAN AMOUNT IS $1000'
        return ws_valid_flag, ws_error_msg
    if ws_loan_amount > 10000000:
        ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        ws_error_msg = 'MAXIMUM LOAN AMOUNT EXCEEDED'
        return ws_valid_flag, ws_error_msg
    if ws_loan_term_months < 6 or ws_loan_term_months > 360:
        ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        ws_error_msg = 'INVALID LOAN TERM'
    return ws_valid_flag, ws_error_msg

def calculate_credit_score(score_payment_history: callable, score_credit_utilization: callable, score_credit_length: callable, score_new_credit: callable, score_credit_mix: callable, determine_tier: callable, ws_credit_score: int) -> None:
    """10200-CALCULATE-CREDIT-SCORE."""
    ws_credit_score = 0
    ws_credit_score = score_payment_history(ws_credit_score)
    ws_credit_score = score_credit_utilization(ws_credit_score)
    ws_credit_score = score_credit_length(ws_credit_score)
    ws_credit_score = score_new_credit(ws_credit_score)
    ws_credit_score = score_credit_mix(ws_credit_score)
    determine_tier(ws_credit_score)

def score_payment_history(on_time_payments: int, late_30_days: int, late_60_days: int, late_90_days: int, ws_credit_score: int) -> int:
    """10210-SCORE-PAYMENT-HISTORY."""
    if (on_time_payments + late_30_days + late_60_days + late_90_days) == 0:
        payment_score = 0  # Avoid division by zero
    else:
        payment_score = (on_time_payments * 100) / (on_time_payments + late_30_days + late_60_days + late_90_days)
    payment_score *= 0.35
    ws_credit_score += int(payment_score)
    return ws_credit_score

def score_credit_utilization(credit_utilization: Decimal, ws_credit_score: int) -> int:
    """10220-SCORE-CREDIT-UTILIZATION."""
    if credit_utilization <= 10:
        util_score = 100
    elif credit_utilization <= 30:
        util_score = 80
    elif credit_utilization <= 50:
        util_score = 60
    elif credit_utilization <= 75:
        util_score = 40
    else:
        util_score = 20
    util_score *= 0.30
    ws_credit_score += int(util_score)
    return ws_credit_score

def score_credit_length(credit_history_len: int, ws_credit_score: int) -> int:
    """10230-SCORE-CREDIT-LENGTH."""
    if credit_history_len >= 84:
        length_score = 100
    elif credit_history_len >= 60:
        length_score = 80
    elif credit_history_len >= 36:
        length_score = 60
    elif credit_history_len >= 12:
        length_score = 40
    else:
        length_score = 20
    length_score *= 0.15
    ws_credit_score += int(length_score)
    return ws_credit_score

def score_new_credit(new_credit_inqs: int, ws_credit_score: int) -> int:
    """10240-SCORE-NEW-CREDIT."""
    if new_credit_in

def calculate_pmi(ws_ltv_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """10335-CALCULATE-PMI."""
    match True:
        case ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0125") / 12
        case ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0100") / 12
        case ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0075") / 12
        case _:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0050") / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount

def evaluate_history(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int,
                         ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:
    """10340-EVALUATE-HISTORY."""
    ws_factor_1 = ""
    ws_factor_2 = ""
    ws_factor_3 = ""
    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk(ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """10350-CALCULATE-FINAL-RISK."""
    ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score // 4
    ws_risk_category = ""
    match True:
        case ws_risk_score >= 80:
            ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
        case ws_risk_score >= 60:
            ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
        case ws_risk_score >= 40:
            ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
        case _:
            ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_risk_category

def determine_approval(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: Decimal,
                         ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal]:
    """10400-DETERMINE-APPROVAL."""
    ws_approval_status = ""
    ws_conditions = ""
    ws_approved_amount = Decimal(0)
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal(0)
    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate

    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    ws_approved_rate = calculate_approved_terms(ws_credit_tier, ws_base_rate, ws_risk_category)
    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate

def calculate_approved_terms(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_base_rate: Decimal, ws_risk_category: str) -> Decimal:
    """10450-CALCULATE-APPROVED-TERMS."""
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal(0)
    match ws_credit_tier:
        case 'A':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.00")
        case 'B':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.50")
        case 'C':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("1.50")
        case 'D':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("3.00")
    if ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        ws_approved_rate += Decimal("0.50")
    return ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms(ws_approved_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """10500-GENERATE-LOAN-TERMS."""
    ws_loan_interest_rate = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    ws_loan_principal_bal = ws_loan_amount
    return ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_monthly_rate, ws_compound_factor, ws_loan_monthly_pmt

def create_amortization(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal,
                        loan_mortgage: bool, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal,
                        amort_interest: list[Decimal], amort_principal: list[Decimal], amort_balance: list[Decimal],
                        amort_payment_num: list[int], amort_payment_amt: list[Decimal], amort_escrow: list[Decimal],
                        amort_total_pmt: list[Decimal], amort_payment_date: list[int]) -> tuple[list[Decimal], list[Decimal], list[Decimal], list[int], list[Decimal], list[Decimal], list[Decimal], list[int]]:
    """10600-CREATE-AMORTIZATION."""
    ws_running_balance = ws_loan_amount
    ws_payment_date = date.today()
    ws_payment_month = ws_payment_date.month
    ws_payment_year = ws_payment_date.year

    for ws_amort_idx in range(1, ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        amort_interest[ws_amort_idx-1], amort_principal[ws_amort_idx-1], ws_running_balance, amort_balance[ws_amort_idx-1], amort_payment_num[ws_amort_idx-1], amort_payment_amt[ws_amort_idx-1], amort_escrow[ws_amort_idx-1], amort_total_pmt[ws_amort_idx-1] = calculate_payment_split(ws_running_balance, ws_monthly_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt,
                                                           loan_mortgage, ws_property_tax, ws_insurance_premium, ws_pmi_amount, ws_amort_idx)
        ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year, amort_payment_date[ws_amort_idx-1] = advance_payment_date(ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year, ws_amort_idx)

    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_escrow, amort_total_pmt, amort_payment_date

def calculate_payment_split(ws_running_balance: Decimal, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal,
                                loan_mortgage: bool, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, ws_amort_idx: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, int, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """10650-CALCULATE-PAYMENT-SPLIT."""

    amort_interest = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
    amort_principal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    ws_running_balance -= amort_principal
    amort_balance = ws_running_balance
    amort_payment_num = ws_amort_idx
    amort_payment_amt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    if loan_mortgage:
        amort_escrow = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow + ws_pmi_amount
    else:
        amort_escrow = Decimal(0)
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt

    return amort_interest, amort_principal, ws_running_balance, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_escrow, amort_total_pmt

def advance_payment_date(ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int, ws_amort_idx: int) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    """10660-ADVANCE-PAYMENT-DATE."""
    ws_payment_month += 1
    if ws_payment_month > 12:
        ws_payment_month = 1
        ws_payment_year += 1
    amort_payment_date = ws_payment_year * 10000 + ws_payment_month * 100 + 1
    return ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year, amort_payment_date

def finalize_loan(ws_loan_term_months: int) -> tuple[int, int, str]:
    """10700-FINALIZE-LOAN."""
    ws_loan_start_date = int(date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d"))
    ws_loan_end_date = ws_loan_start_date + (ws_loan_term_months * 30)
    ws_loan_status = 'A'
    # Calls to 10750, 10760, 10770 are omitted here as they involve external operations
    return ws_loan_start_date, ws_loan_end_date, ws_loan_status

    ws_loan_record.loan_rec_type = ws_loan_type
    ws_loan_record.loan_rec_amount = ws_loan_amount
    ws_loan_record.loan_rec_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate
    ws_loan_record.loan_rec_payment = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    ws_loan_record.loan_rec_start = ws_loan_start_date
    ws_loan_record.loan_rec_status = ws_loan_status
    # Omit WRITE operation as it depends on external context.
    return ws_loan_record

def portfolio_management():
    """11000-PORTFOLIO-MANAGEMENT."""
    load_portfolio()
    update_market_prices()
    calculate_values()
    rebalance_check()
    generate_statements()

def load_portfolio() -> tuple[list[Holding], int]:
    """11100-LOAD-PORTFOLIO."""
    ws_hold_idx = 1
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    ws_holding = []
    ws_holdings_count = 0
    # The following code simulates file reading, replace it with actual implementation
    holdings_data = [
        Holding("AAPL", 100, Decimal("150.00"), Decimal("170.00"), type="STK"),
        Holding("GOOG", 50, Decimal("2500.00"), Decimal("2700.00"), type="STK"),
        Holding("BND1", 200, Decimal("100.00"), Decimal("102.00"), type="BND"),
    ]  # Example holdings data

    for ws_holding_rec in holdings_data:  # Assuming holdings_data represents data from HOLDINGS-FILE
        if ws_hold_idx > 100 or ws_eof_flag == 'Y':
            break
        ws_holding.append(ws_holding_rec)  #MOVE WS-HOLDING-REC TO WS-HOLDING(WS-HOLD-IDX)
        ws_hold_idx += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-HOLD-IDX

    ws_holdings_count = ws_hold_idx - 1  #SUBTRACT 1 FROM WS-HOLD-IDX GIVING WS-HOLDINGS-COUNT
    return ws_holding, ws_holdings_count

def update_market_prices(ws_holding: list[Holding], ws_holdings_count: int) -> list[Holding]:
    """11200-UPDATE-MARKET-PRICES."""
    for ws_hold_idx in range(ws_holdings_count):
        ws_quote_symbol = ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].symbol #MOVE HOLD-SYMBOL(WS-HOLD-IDX) TO WS-QUOTE-SYMBOL
        ws_quote_price = get_quote(ws_quote_symbol) #PERFORM 11250-GET-QUOTE
        ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].current_price = ws_quote_price  #MOVE WS-QUOTE-PRICE TO HOLD-CURRENT-PRICE(WS-HOLD-IDX)
    return ws_holding

def get_quote(ws_quote_symbol: str) -> Decimal:
    """11250-GET-QUOTE."""
    # In a real implementation, this function would call an external API
    # Here, we simulate the API call with a dictionary of prices
    quote_data = {"AAPL": Decimal("175.00"), "GOOG": Decimal("2750.00"), "BND1": Decimal("102.50")}
    quote_request_symbol = ws_quote_symbol
    try:
        ws_quote_price = quote_data[quote_request_symbol]
        quote_response_status = 'OK'
    except KeyError:
        ws_quote_price = Decimal("0.00")
        quote_response_status = 'ERROR'
    if quote_response_status != 'OK':
        ws_quote_price = Decimal("0.00")
    return ws_quote_price

def calculate_values(ws_holding: list[Holding], ws_holdings_count: int) -> tuple[list[Holding], Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """11300-CALCULATE-VALUES."""
    ws_total_value = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_cost_basis = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_unrealized_gain = Decimal("0.00")
    for ws_hold_idx in range(ws_holdings_count):
        ws_holding[ws_hold_idx], ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain = calculate_holding_value(ws_holding[ws_hold_idx], ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain) #PERFORM 11350-CALCULATE-HOLDING-VALUE

    return ws_holding, ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain

def calculate_holding_value(holding: Holding, ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_cost_basis: Decimal, ws_unrealized_gain: Decimal) -> tuple[Holding, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """11350-CALCULATE-HOLDING-VALUE."""
    holding.market_value = holding.shares * holding.current_price  #COMPUTE HOLD-MARKET-VALUE(WS-HOLD-IDX)
    ws_hold_cost = holding.shares * holding.cost_per_share  #COMPUTE WS-HOLD-COST
    holding.gain_loss = holding.market_value - ws_hold_cost  #COMPUTE HOLD-GAIN-LOSS(WS-HOLD-IDX)
    if ws_hold_cost > 0:
        holding.pct_change = (holding.gain_loss / ws_hold_cost) * 100  #COMPUTE HOLD-PCT-CHANGE(WS-HOLD-IDX)
    else:
        holding.pct_change = Decimal("0.00")  #MOVE ZEROES TO HOLD-PCT-CHANGE(WS-HOLD-IDX)

    ws_total_value += holding.market_value #ADD HOLD-MARKET-VALUE(WS-HOLD-IDX) TO WS-TOTAL-VALUE
    ws_cost_basis += ws_hold_cost  #ADD WS-HOLD-COST TO WS-COST-BASIS
    ws_unrealized_gain += holding.gain_loss #ADD HOLD-GAIN-LOSS(WS-HOLD-IDX) TO WS-UNREALIZED-GAIN

    return holding, ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain

def rebalance_check(ws_holding: list[Holding], ws_holdings_count: int, ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_bonds_pct: Decimal) -> None:
    """11400-REBALANCE-CHECK."""
    ws_rebalance_needed, ws_stocks_diff = calculate_current_allocation(ws_holding, ws_holdings_count, ws_total_value, ws_target_stocks_pct, ws_target_bonds_pct) #PERFORM 11410-CALCULATE-CURRENT-ALLOCATION
    ws_rebalance_needed = compare_to_target(ws_stocks_diff)  #PERFORM 11420-COMPARE-TO-TARGET
    if ws_rebalance_needed == 'Y':
        generate_rebalance_trades(ws_total_value, ws_stocks_diff)

def calculate_current_allocation(ws_holding: list[Holding], ws_holdings_count: int, ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_bonds_pct: Decimal) -> tuple[str, Decimal]:
    """11410-CALCULATE-CURRENT-ALLOCATION."""
    ws_stocks_value = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_bonds_value = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_cash_value = Decimal("0.00")

    for ws_hold_idx in range(ws_holdings_count):
        match ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].type:
            case 'STK':
                ws_stocks_value += ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].market_value
            case 'BND':
                ws_bonds_value += ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].market_value
            case 'CSH':
                ws_cash_value += ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].market_value

    ws_stocks_pct = (ws_stocks_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_bonds_pct = (ws_bonds_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_cash_pct = (ws_cash_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_stocks_diff = ws_stocks_pct - ws_target_stocks_pct
    return 'N', ws_stocks_diff

def compare_to_target(ws_stocks_diff: Decimal) -> str:
    """11420-COMPARE-TO-TARGET."""
    ws_rebalance_needed = 'N'
    if abs(ws_stocks_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    return ws_rebalance_needed

def generate_rebalance_trades(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_stocks_diff: Decimal):
    """11430-GENERATE-REBALANCE-TRADES."""
    if ws_stocks_diff > 0:
        ws_sell_amount = ws_total_value * ws_stocks_diff / 100
        create_sell_order(ws_sell_amount)
    else:
        ws_buy_amount = ws_total_value * (0 - ws_stocks_diff) / 100
        create_buy_order(ws_buy_amount)

def create_sell_order(ws_sell_amount: Decimal):
    """11440-CREATE-SELL-ORDER."""
    ws_trade_type = 'SELL'
    ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
    ws_trade_amount = ws_sell_amount
    trade_execution(ws_trade_type, ws_order_type, ws_trade_amount)

def create_buy_order(ws_buy_amount: Decimal):
    """11450-CREATE-BUY-ORDER."""
    ws_trade_type = 'BUY '
    ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
    ws_trade_amount = ws_buy_amount
    trade_execution(ws_trade_type, ws_order_type, ws_trade_amount)

def generate_statements(ws_end_of_quarter: str, ws_end_of_year: str, ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_quarter_start_value: Decimal, ws_dividend_income: Decimal, ws_realized_gain_ytd: Decimal, ws_holding: list[Holding], ws_holdings_count: int):

    if ws_end_of_year == 'Y':
        annual_tax_report(ws_dividend_income, ws_realized_gain_ytd)

def quarterly_report(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_quarter_start_value: Decimal):
    """11520-QUARTERLY-REPORT."""
    rpt_title = 'QUARTERLY PERFORMANCE REPORT'
    rpt_quarter_return = (ws_total_value - ws_quarter_start_value) / ws_quarter_start_value * 100
    #WRITE REPORT-RECORD FROM WS-PERFORMANCE-LINE.

def annual_tax_report(ws_dividend_income: Decimal, ws_realized_gain_ytd: Decimal):
    """11530-ANNUAL-TAX-REPORT."""
    rpt_title = 'ANNUAL TAX REPORT - 1099'
    rpt_dividends = ws_dividend_income
    rpt_cap_gains = ws_realized_gain_ytd
    #WRITE REPORT-RECORD FROM WS-TAX-LINE.

def trade_execution(ws_trade_type: str, ws_order_type: str, ws_trade_amount: Decimal):
    """12000-TRADE-EXECUTION."""
    ws_trade_symbol = "AAPL" # Mocking a symbol
    ws_trade_shares = 10 # Mocking shares
    ws_limit_price = Decimal("176.00") # Mocking a price
    ws_stop_price = Decimal("174.00")
    ws_estimated_price = Decimal("175.50")
    ws_available_cash = Decimal("10000.00")
    ws_current_market_price = Decimal("175.75")
    ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason = validate_order(ws_trade_symbol, ws_trade_shares, ws_limit_price) #Perform 12100-VALIDATE-ORDER
    if ws_order_valid == 'Y':
        ws_sufficient_flag, ws_reject_reason = check_funds_shares(ws_trade_type, ws_trade_shares, ws_estimated_price, ws_available_cash, ws_trade_symbol)
        if ws_sufficient_flag == 'Y':
            ws_routing_type = route_order(ws_trade_amount) # Perform 12300-ROUTE-ORDER
            ws_trade_status, ws_executed_price = execute_order(ws_order_type, ws_limit_price, ws_stop_price, ws_trade_type, ws_current_market_price) #Perform 12400-EXECUTE-ORDER
            if ws_trade_status == 'FILLED':
                settle_trade(ws_trade_shares, ws_executed_price, ws_trade_type, ws_available_cash, ws_trade_symbol) #Perform 12500-SETTLE-TRADE
        else:
            reject_order(ws_reject_reason) # Perform 12600-REJECT-ORDER

def validate_order(ws_trade_symbol: str, ws_trade_shares: int, ws_limit_price: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """12100-VALIDATE-ORDER."""
    ws_order_valid = 'Y'
    ws_reject_reason = ''
    if ws_trade_symbol == '':
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'SYMBOL REQUIRED'
        return ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason
    if ws_trade_shares <= 0:
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'INVALID QUANTITY'
        return ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason
    if ws_limit_price > 0: #ORDER-LIMIT OR ORDER-STOP-LIMIT (interpreted as any non zero price)
        if ws_limit_price <= 0:
            ws_order_valid = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'LIMIT PRICE REQUIRED'
    return ws_order_valid, ws_reject_reason

def check_funds_shares(ws_trade_type: str, ws_trade_shares: int, ws_estimated_price: Decimal, ws_available_cash: Decimal, ws_trade_symbol: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """12200-CHECK-FUNDS-SHARES."""
    ws_sufficient_flag = 'Y'
    ws_reject_reason = ''
    if ws_trade_type == 'BUY ': #TRADE-BUY
        ws_required_funds = ws_trade_shares * ws_estimated_price
        if ws_required_funds > ws_available_cash:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
            return ws_sufficient_flag, ws_reject_reason
    if ws_trade_type == 'SELL': #TRADE-SELL
        ws_current_shares = check_share_position(ws_trade_symbol)
        if ws_current_shares < ws_trade_shares:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT SHARES'
            return ws_sufficient_flag, ws_reject_reason
    return ws_sufficient_flag, ws_reject_reason

def check_share_position(ws_trade_symbol: str) -> int:
    """12250-CHECK-SHARE-POSITION."""
    ws_current_shares = 0
    ws_holding, ws_holdings_count = load_portfolio()
    for hold in ws_holding:
        if hold.symbol == ws_trade_symbol:
            ws_current_shares += hold.shares
    return ws_current_shares

def route_order(ws_trade_amount: Decimal) -> str:
    """12300-ROUTE-ORDER."""
    match True:
        case ws_trade_amount > 100000:
            ws_routing_type = 'ALGO'
        case ws_trade_amount > 10000:
            ws_routing_type = 'SMART'
        case _:
            ws_routing_type = 'DIRECT'
    ws_order_time = date.today()

def move(source: any, destination: any) -> any:
    """Simulates COBOL MOVE statement"""
    return source

def initialize(record: object) -> None:
    """Simulates COBOL INITIALIZE statement"""
    for field in record.__dataclass_fields__:
        setattr(record, field, None)

def string(*args, delimiter: str, into: str) -> str:
    """Simulates COBOL STRING statement"""
    result = ""
    for arg in args:
        result += str(arg).split(delimiter)[0]
    return result

    return 0.5  # Replace with actual random logic

    """Simulates COBOL FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE"""
    return 1000  # Replace with actual date conversion logic

def add(value: Decimal, target: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Simulates COBOL ADD statement"""
    return target + value

def compute(expression: str) -> Decimal:
    """Simulates COBOL COMPUTE statement"""
    # This is a simplified example and might need more complex parsing
    try:
        return eval(expression)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error evaluating expression: {expression}, error: {e}")
        return Decimal("0.00")

def perform(procedure: callable) -> None:
    """Simulates COBOL PERFORM statement"""
    procedure()

def call(program_name: str, *args):
    """Simulates COBOL CALL statement"""
    print(f"Calling {program_name} with args: {args}")

def write(record_name: str, data: object) -> None:
    """Simulates COBOL WRITE statement"""
    print(f"Writing {record_name} from {data}")

def rewrite(record_name: str) -> None:
    """Simulates COBOL REWRITE statement"""
    print(f"Rewriting {record_name}")

def read(file_name: str, key: str) -> str:
    """Simulates COBOL READ statement"""
    print(f"Reading {file_name} with key {key}")
    return "Record Data"

def handle_error() -> None:
    """Simulates COBOL error handling"""
    print("Handling Error")

def vary(index: str, start: int, increment: int, until: str) -> None:
    """Simulates COBOL VARYING statement"""
    print(f"VARYING {index} FROM {start} BY {increment} UNTIL {until}")

def subtract(value: int, target: int) -> int:
    """Simulates COBOL SUBTRACT statement"""
    return target - value

def _16115_end_if() -> None:
    """
    16115-END-IF.
        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PEP-STATUS
        MOVE PEP-MATCH-SCORE TO WS-PEP-SCORE
    END-IF.
    """
    global WS_PEP_STATUS, WS_PEP_SCORE, PEP_MATCH_SCORE
    WS_PEP_STATUS = 'Y'
    WS_PEP_SCORE = PEP_MATCH_SCORE

def _16116_check_adverse_media() -> None:
    """
    16116-CHECK-ADVERSE-MEDIA.
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO MEDIA-SEARCH-NAME
        CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING MEDIA-REQUEST MEDIA-RESPONSE
        IF MEDIA-HITS-FOUND > 0
           ADD MEDIA-HITS-FOUND TO WS-WATCHLIST-HITS
        END-IF.
    """
    global WS_CUSTOMER_NAME, MEDIA_SEARCH_NAME, MEDIA_REQUEST, MEDIA_RESPONSE, MEDIA_HITS_FOUND, WS_WATCHLIST_HITS
    MEDIA_SEARCH_NAME = WS_CUSTOMER_NAME
    call('MEDIASRCH', MEDIA_REQUEST, MEDIA_RESPONSE)
    if MEDIA_HITS_FOUND > 0:
        WS_WATCHLIST_HITS += MEDIA_HITS_FOUND

def _16120_calculate_match_score() -> None:
    """
    16120-CALCULATE-MATCH-SCORE.
        IF WS-OFAC-SCORE > 0
           ADD WS-OFAC-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
        END-IF
        IF WS-PEP-SCORE > 0
           ADD WS-PEP-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
        END-IF
        COMPUTE WS-MATCH-SCORE =
           WS-MATCH-SCORE / WS-WATCHLIST-HITS.
    """
    global WS_OFAC_SCORE, WS_MATCH_SCORE, WS_PEP_SCORE, WS_WATCHLIST_HITS
    if WS_OFAC_SCORE > 0:
        WS_MATCH_SCORE += WS_OFAC_SCORE
    if WS_PEP_SCORE > 0:
        WS_MATCH_SCORE += WS_PEP_SCORE
    if WS_WATCHLIST_HITS != 0:
        WS_MATCH_SCORE = WS_MATCH_SCORE / WS_WATCHLIST_HITS
    else:
        WS_MATCH_SCORE = Decimal("0.00")

def _16130_determine_disposition() -> None:
    """
    16130-DETERMINE-DISPOSITION.
        EVALUATE TRUE
           WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 90
              MOVE 'CONFIRMED' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-SAR-REQUIRED
           WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 75
              MOVE 'POTENTIAL' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
              MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
           WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 50
              MOVE 'WEAK' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
              MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
           WHEN OTHER
              MOVE 'FALSE POSITIVE' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
              MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
        END-EVALUATE.
    """
    global WS_MATCH_SCORE, WS_MATCH_TYPE, WS_SAR_REQUIRED, WS_CASE_STATUS
    if WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 90:
        WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'CONFIRMED'
        WS_SAR_REQUIRED = 'Y'
    elif WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 75:
        WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'POTENTIAL'
        WS_CASE_STATUS = 'REVIEW'
    elif WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 50:
        WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'WEAK'
        WS_CASE_STATUS = 'CLEARED'
    else:
        WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
        WS_CASE_STATUS = 'CLEARED'

def _16200_kyc_verification() -> None:
    """
    16200-KYC-VERIFICATION.
        PERFORM 16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY
        PERFORM 16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS
        PERFORM 16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS
        PERFORM 16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS.
    """
    _16210_verify_identity()
    _16220_verify_address()
    _16230_verify_documents()
    _16240_determine_kyc_status()

def _16210_verify_identity() -> None:
    """
    16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY.
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO ID-VERIFY-SSN
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-DOB TO ID-VERIFY-DOB
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO ID-VERIFY-NAME
        CALL 'IDVERIFY' USING ID-REQUEST ID-RESPONSE
        IF ID-VERIFIED = 'Y'
           MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
        ELSE
           MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
        END-IF.
    """
    global WS_CUSTOMER_SSN, ID_VERIFY_SSN, WS_CUSTOMER_DOB, ID_VERIFY_DOB, WS_CUSTOMER_NAME, ID_VERIFY_NAME, ID_REQUEST, ID_RESPONSE, ID_VERIFIED, WS_ID_STATUS
    ID_VERIFY_SSN = WS_CUSTOMER_SSN
    ID_VERIFY_DOB = WS_CUSTOMER_DOB
    ID_VERIFY_NAME = WS_CUSTOMER_NAME
    call('IDVERIFY', ID_REQUEST, ID_RESPONSE)
    if ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':
        WS_ID_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        WS_ID_STATUS = 'FAILED'

def _16220_verify_address() -> None:
    """
    16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS.
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO ADDR-VERIFY-INPUT
        CALL 'ADDRVERIFY' USING ADDR-REQUEST ADDR-RESPONSE
        IF ADDR-VERIFIED = 'Y'
           MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
        ELSE
           MOVE 'UNVERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
        END-IF.
    """
    global WS_CUSTOMER_ADDRESS, ADDR_VERIFY_INPUT, ADDR_REQUEST, ADDR_RESPONSE, ADDR_VERIFIED, WS_ADDR_STATUS
    ADDR_VERIFY_INPUT = WS_CUSTOMER_ADDRESS
    call('ADDRVERIFY', ADDR_REQUEST, ADDR_RESPONSE)
    if ADDR_VERIFIED == 'Y':
        WS_ADDR_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        WS_ADDR_STATUS = 'UNVERIFIED'

def _16230_verify_documents() -> None:
    """
    16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS.
        IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'PASSPORT'
           PERFORM 16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT
        ELSE IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'LICENSE'
           PERFORM 16234-VERIFY-LICENSE
        ELSE
           PERFORM 16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC
        END-IF
        END-IF.
    """
    global WS_DOC_TYPE
    if WS_DOC_TYPE == 'PASSPORT':
        _16232_verify_passport()
    elif WS_DOC_TYPE == 'LICENSE':
        _16234_verify_license()
    else:
        _16236_verify_other_doc()

def _16232_verify_passport() -> None:
    """
    16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT.
        MOVE WS-PASSPORT-NUMBER TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-NUM
        MOVE WS-PASSPORT-COUNTRY TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-COUNTRY
        CALL 'PASSVERIFY' USING PASSPORT-REQ PASSPORT-RESP
        IF PASSPORT-VALID = 'Y'
           MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
        ELSE
           MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
        END-IF.
    """
    global WS_PASSPORT_NUMBER, PASSPORT_VERIFY_NUM, WS_PASSPORT_COUNTRY, PASSPORT_VERIFY_COUNTRY, PASSPORT_REQ, PASSPORT_RESP, PASSPORT_VALID, WS_DOC_STATUS
    PASSPORT_VERIFY_NUM = WS_PASSPORT_NUMBER
    PASSPORT_VERIFY_COUNTRY = WS_PASSPORT_COUNTRY
    call('PASSVERIFY', PASSPORT_REQ, PASSPORT_RESP)
    if PASSPORT_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_DOC_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        WS_DOC_STATUS = 'INVALID'

def _16240_determine_kyc_status() -> None:
    """
    16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS.
        IF WS-ID-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND
           WS-ADDR-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND
           WS-DOC-STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
           MOVE 'APPROVED' TO WS-KYC-STATUS
        ELSE
           MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-KYC-STATUS
        END-IF.
    """
    global WS_ID_STATUS, WS_ADDR_STATUS, WS_DOC_STATUS, WS_KYC_STATUS
    if WS_ID_STATUS == 'VERIFIED' and WS_ADDR_STATUS == 'VERIFIED' and WS_DOC_STATUS == 'VERIFIED':
        WS_KYC_STATUS = 'APPROVED'
    else:
        WS_KYC_STATUS = 'PENDING'

def _16300_sanctions_check() -> None:
    """
    16300-SANCTIONS-CHECK.

    global WS_SANCTIONS_HIT
    if WS_SANCTIONS_HIT == 'Y':
        _16310_escalate_to_compliance()
        _16320_freeze_account()

def _16310_escalate_to_compliance() -> None:
    """
    16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE.
        INITIALIZE WS-ESCALATION-RECORD
        MOVE 'SANCTIONS HIT' TO ESC-REASON
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ID TO ESC-CUSTOMER
        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ESC-DATE
        MOVE 'URGENT' TO ESC-PRIORITY
        WRITE ESCALATION-RECORD FROM WS-ESCALATION-RECORD.
    """
    global WS_ESCALATION_RECORD, ESC_REASON, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, ESC_DATE, ESC_PRIORITY
    WS_ESCALATION_RECORD = EscalationRecord()
    ESC_REASON = 'SANCTIONS HIT'
    ESC_CUSTOMER = WS_CUSTOMER_ID
    ESC_DATE = function_current_date()
    ESC_PRIORITY = 'URGENT'
    write('ESCALATION-RECORD', WS_ESCALATION_RECORD)

def _16320_freeze_account() -> None:
    """
    16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT.
        MOVE 'F' TO WS-ACCOUNT-STATUS
        MOVE 'SANCTIONS FREEZE' TO WS-FREEZE-REASON
        REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD.
    """
    global WS_ACCOUNT_STATUS, WS_FREEZE_REASON
    WS_ACCOUNT_STATUS = 'F'
    WS_FREEZE_REASON = 'SANCTIONS FREEZE'
    rewrite('ACCOUNT-RECORD')

def _16410_check_velocity() -> None:
    """
    16410-CHECK-VELOCITY.
        IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-COUNT > WS-VELOCITY-THRESHOLD
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VELOCITY-FLAG
           ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF
        IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT > WS-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AMOUNT-FLAG
           ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF.
    """
    global WS_DAILY_TRANS_COUNT, WS_VELOCITY_THRESHOLD, WS_VELOCITY_FLAG, WS_FRAUD_SCORE, WS_DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_AMOUNT_THRESHOLD, WS_AMOUNT_FLAG
    if WS_DAILY_TRANS_COUNT > WS_VELOCITY_THRESHOLD:
        WS_VELOCITY_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 20
    if WS_DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT > WS_AMOUNT_THRESHOLD:
        WS_AMOUNT_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 20

def _16420_check_patterns() -> None:
    """
    16420-CHECK-PATTERNS.
        IF WS-ROUND-AMOUNT-COUNT > 5
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
           ADD 15 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF
        IF WS-STRUCTURING-DETECTED = 'Y'
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
           ADD 30 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF.
    """
    global WS_ROUND_AMOUNT_COUNT, WS_PATTERN_FLAG, WS_FRAUD_SCORE, WS_STRUCTURING_DETECTED
    if WS_ROUND_AMOUNT_COUNT > 5:
        WS_PATTERN_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 15
    if WS_STRUCTURING_DETECTED == 'Y':
        WS_PATTERN_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 30

def _16430_check_high_risk() -> None:
    """
    16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK.
        IF WS-HIGH-RISK-COUNTRY = 'Y'
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-LOCATION-FLAG
           ADD 25 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF
        IF WS-NEW-DEVICE = 'Y'
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DEVICE-FLAG
           ADD 10 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF.
    """
    global WS_HIGH_RISK_COUNTRY, WS_LOCATION_FLAG, WS_FRAUD_SCORE, WS_NEW_DEVICE, WS_DEVICE_FLAG
    if WS_HIGH_RISK_COUNTRY == 'Y':
        WS_LOCATION_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 25
    if WS_NEW_DEVICE == 'Y':
        WS_DEVICE_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 10

def _16440_calculate_risk_score() -> None:
    """
    16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE.
        EVALUATE TRUE
           WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 80
              MOVE 'BLOCK' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
           WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 60
              MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
           WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 40
              MOVE 'MONITOR' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
           WHEN OTHER
              MOVE 'APPROVE' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
        END-EVALUATE.
    """
    global WS_FRAUD_SCORE, WS_FRAUD_DECISION, WS_MANUAL_REVIEW
    if WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 80:
        WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'BLOCK'
        WS_MANUAL_REVIEW = 'Y'
    elif WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 60:
        WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'REVIEW'
        WS_MANUAL_REVIEW = 'Y'
    elif WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 40:
        WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'MONITOR'
    else:
        WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'APPROVE'
