    """Placeholder for 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION"""
    print(f"Sending notification: {notif_type}, {notif_channel}, {notif_subject}")

def pinverify(card_number: str, current_pin: str) -> str:
    """Placeholder for PINVERIFY call"""
    if current_pin == "1234":
        return 'MATCH'
    else:
        return 'NO MATCH'

def pinencrypt(new_pin: str) -> str:
    """Placeholder for PINENCRYPT call"""
    return f"ENCRYPTED({new_pin})"

def random() -> float:
    """Placeholder for RANDOM"""
    return 0.5

def calculate_luhn_check(card_number_temp: str) -> int:
    """Placeholder for calculate luhn check."""
    luhn_sum = 0
    for i in range(15, 0, -1):
        luhn_digit = int(card_number_temp[i - 1])
        if (16 - i) % 2 == 0:
            luhn_digit *= 2
            if luhn_digit > 9:
                luhn_digit -= 9
        luhn_sum += luhn_digit
    return (10 - luhn_sum % 10) % 10

def calculate_next_run_date(ws: WSRecords, frequency: str) -> None:
    """Original COBOL lines:
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 1
              WHEN 'WEEKLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 7
              WHEN 'MONTHLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 30
              WHEN 'QUARTERLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 90
              WHEN 'YEARLY'
                 COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = 
                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 365
           END-EVALUATE."""
    match frequency:
        case 'DAILY':
            ws.ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date(ws.ws_last_run_date) + 1
        case 'WEEKLY':
            ws.ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date(ws.ws_last_run_date) + 7
        case 'MONTHLY':
            ws.ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date(ws.ws_last_run_date) + 30
        case 'QUARTERLY':
            ws.ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date(ws.ws_last_run_date) + 90
        case 'YEARLY':
            ws.ws_next_run_date = integer_of_date(ws.ws_last_run_date) + 365

def data_analytics(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21000-DATA-ANALYTICS."""
    collect_metrics(ws)
    aggregate_data(ws)
    calculate_kpi(ws)
    generate_dashboard(ws)
    export_data(ws)

def collect_metrics(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21100-COLLECT-METRICS."""
    collect_transaction_metrics(ws)
    collect_customer_metrics(ws)
    collect_performance_metrics(ws)

def collect_transaction_metrics(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS."""
    ws.ws_total_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
    ws.ws_total_trans_count = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
    ws.ws_avg_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT
    transactions = [
        TransactionRecord(trans_amount=Decimal("100.00")),
        TransactionRecord(trans_amount=Decimal("200.00")),
        TransactionRecord(trans_amount=Decimal("150.00"))
    ]
    for trans in transactions: # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        # READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANS-REC
        # AT END MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        # NOT AT END
        ws.ws_total_trans_count += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
        ws.ws_total_trans_amount += trans.trans_amount # ADD TRANS-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
    ws.ws_eof_flag = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
    if ws.ws_total_trans_count > 0: # IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
        ws.ws_avg_trans_amount = ws.ws_total_trans_amount / ws.ws_total_trans_count # COMPUTE WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT = WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT

def collect_customer_metrics(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS."""
    ws.ws_active_customers = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
    ws.ws_new_customers = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
    ws.ws_churned_customers = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS

    customers = [
        CustomerRecord(cust_status='A', cust_open_date='2023-12-01', cust_close_date=''),
        CustomerRecord(cust_status='I', cust_open_date='2024-01-15', cust_close_date=''),
        CustomerRecord(cust_status='A', cust_open_date='2024-02-01', cust_close_date='2024-02-15')
    ]

    for cust in customers: # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        # READ CUSTOMER-FILE INTO WS-CUST-REC
        # AT END MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        # NOT AT END
        if cust.cust_status == 'A': # IF CUST-STATUS = 'A'
            ws.ws_active_customers += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
        if cust.cust_open_date >= ws.ws_process_date: # IF CUST-OPEN-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
            ws.ws_new_customers += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
        if cust.cust_close_date >= ws.ws_process_date: # IF CUST-CLOSE-DATE >= WS-PERIOD-START
            ws.ws_churned_customers += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS

    ws.ws_eof_flag = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def collect_performance_metrics(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS."""
    ws.ws_response_time_total = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
    ws.ws_response_count = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT

    performance_logs = [
        PerformanceLogRecord(perf_response_time=Decimal("0.5")),
        PerformanceLogRecord(perf_response_time=Decimal("0.7")),
        PerformanceLogRecord(perf_response_time=Decimal("0.6"))
    ]

    for perf in performance_logs: # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        # READ PERF-LOG-FILE INTO WS-PERF-REC
        # AT END MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        # NOT AT END
        ws.ws_response_time_total += perf.perf_response_time # ADD PERF-RESPONSE-TIME TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
        ws.ws_response_count += 1 # ADD 1 TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT

    if ws.ws_response_count > 0: # IF WS-RESPONSE-COUNT > 0
        ws.ws_avg_response_time = ws.ws_response_time_total / ws.ws_response_count # COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE-TIME = WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL / WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
    
    ws.ws_eof_flag = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def aggregate_data(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21200-AGGREGATE-DATA."""
    daily_aggregation(ws)
    weekly_aggregation(ws)
    monthly_aggregation(ws)

def daily_aggregation(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION."""
    ws.ws_daily_summary = DailySummary() # INITIALIZE WS-DAILY-SUMMARY
    ws.ws_daily_summary.daily_date = ws.ws_process_date # MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO DAILY-DATE
    ws.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count = ws.ws_total_trans_count # MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-COUNT
    ws.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount = ws.ws_total_trans_amount # MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT
    ws.ws_daily_summary.daily_deposits = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO DAILY-DEPOSITS (assuming it's zero for now)
    ws.ws_daily_summary.daily_withdrawals = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO DAILY-WITHDRAWALS (assuming it's zero for now)
    # WRITE DAILY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-DAILY-SUMMARY
    print(f"Writing daily summary: {ws.ws_daily_summary}")

def weekly_aggregation(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION."""
    if ws.ws_day_of_week == 7: # IF WS-DAY-OF-WEEK = 7
        ws.ws_weekly_summary = WeeklySummary() # INITIALIZE WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
        ws.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_week = ws.ws_week_number # MOVE WS-WEEK-NUMBER TO WEEKLY-WEEK
        sum_week_data(ws) # PERFORM 21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA
        # WRITE WEEKLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
        print(f"Writing weekly summary: {ws.ws_weekly_summary}")

def sum_week_data(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA."""
    if ws.ws_weekly_summary is None:
        ws.ws_weekly_summary = WeeklySummary()
    ws.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
    ws.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
    for _ in range(7): # PERFORM 7 TIMES
        if ws.ws_daily_summary is not None:
            ws.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count += ws.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count # ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
            ws.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount += ws.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount # ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT

def monthly_aggregation(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION."""
    if ws.ws_end_of_month == 'Y': # IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
        ws.ws_monthly_summary = MonthlySummary() # INITIALIZE WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
        ws.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_month = ws.ws_curr_month # MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO MONTHLY-MONTH
        ws.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_year = ws.ws_curr_year # MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR TO MONTHLY-YEAR
        sum_month_data(ws) # PERFORM 21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA
        # WRITE MONTHLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
        print(f"Writing monthly summary: {ws.ws_monthly_summary}")

def sum_month_data(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA."""
    if ws.ws_monthly_summary is None:
        ws.ws_monthly_summary = MonthlySummary()
    ws.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
    ws.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
    ws.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_new_accounts = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-NEW-ACCOUNTS
    ws.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_closed_accounts = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-CLOSED-ACCOUNTS

    daily_summaries = [
        DailySummary(daily_month=1, daily_trans_count=10, daily_trans_amount=Decimal("100.00")),
        DailySummary(daily_month=1, daily_trans_count=15, daily_trans_amount=Decimal("150.00")),
        DailySummary(daily_month=2, daily_trans_count=20, daily_trans_amount=Decimal("200.00"))
    ]

    ws.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    for daily_sum in daily_summaries: # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
        # READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
        # AT END MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        # NOT AT END
        if daily_sum.daily_month == ws.ws_curr_month: # IF DAILY-MONTH = WS-CURR-MONTH
            ws.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count += daily_sum.daily_trans_count # ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
            ws.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount += daily_sum.daily_trans_amount # ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
    ws.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_kpi(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21300-CALCULATE-KPI."""
    calc_financial_kpi(ws)
    calc_operational_kpi(ws)
    calc_customer_kpi(ws)

def calc_financial_kpi(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI."""
    if ws.ws_total_assets > 0: # IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
        ws.ws_roa = (ws.ws_net_income / ws.ws_total_assets) * 100 # COMPUTE WS-ROA = (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100
    if ws.ws_total_equity > 0: # IF WS-TOTAL-EQUITY > 0
        ws.ws_roe = (ws.ws_net_income / ws.ws_total_equity) * 100 # COMPUTE WS-ROE = (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-EQUITY) * 100
    if ws.ws_interest_expense > 0: # IF WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE > 0
        ws.ws_nim = ((ws.ws_interest_income - ws.ws_interest_expense) / ws.ws_earning_assets) * 100 # COMPUTE WS-NIM = ((WS-INTEREST-INCOME - WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE) / WS-EARNING-ASSETS) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI."""
    if ws.ws_total_trans_count_kpi > 0: # IF WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT > 0
        ws.ws_error_rate = (ws.ws_error_count / ws.ws_total_trans_count_kpi) * 100 # COMPUTE WS-ERROR-RATE = (WS-ERROR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT) * 100
    ws.ws_sla_compliance = (ws.ws_within_sla_count / ws.ws_total_cases) * 100 # COMPUTE WS-SLA-COMPLIANCE = (WS-WITHIN-SLA-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CASES) * 100
    ws.ws_first_call_resolution = (ws.ws_fcr_count / ws.ws_total_calls) * 100 # COMPUTE WS-FIRST-CALL-RESOLUTION = (WS-FCR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CALLS) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI."""
    if ws.ws_active_customers > 0: # IF WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS > 0
        ws.ws_churn_rate = (ws.ws_churned_customers / ws.ws_active_customers) * 100 # COMPUTE WS-CHURN-RATE = (WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS / WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS) * 100
    ws.ws_acquisition_cost = ws.ws_marketing_spend / ws.ws_new_customers # COMPUTE WS-ACQUISITION-COST = WS-MARKETING-SPEND / WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
    ws.ws_lifetime_value = ws.ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * ws.ws_avg_customer_tenure # COMPUTE WS-LIFETIME-VALUE = WS-AVG-REVENUE-PER-CUSTOMER * WS-AVG-CUSTOMER-TENURE

def generate_dashboard(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21400-GENERATE-DASHBOARD."""
    create_executive_dashboard(ws)
    create_operations_dashboard(ws)
    create_risk_dashboard(ws)

def create_executive_dashboard(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21410-CREATE-EXECUTIVE-DASHBOARD."""
    ws.ws_exec_dashboard = ExecutiveDashboard()
    ws.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_title = 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD' # MOVE 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD' TO DASH-TITLE
    ws.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_revenue = Decimal("100000.00") # MOVE WS-TOTAL-REVENUE TO DASH-REVENUE
    ws.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_net_income = ws.ws_net_income # MOVE WS-NET-INCOME TO DASH-NET-INCOME
    ws.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_roa = ws.ws_roa # MOVE WS-ROA TO DASH-ROA
    ws.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_roe = ws.ws_roe # MOVE WS-ROE TO DASH-ROE
    ws.ws_exec_dashboard.dash_customers = ws.ws_active_customers # MOVE WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS TO DASH-CUSTOMERS
    # WRITE DASHBOARD-RECORD FROM WS-EXEC-DASHBOARD
    print(f"Writing executive dashboard: {ws.ws_exec_dashboard}")

def create_operations_dashboard(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21420-CREATE-OPERATIONS-DASHBOARD."""
    ws.ws_ops_dashboard = OperationsDashboard()
    ws.ws_ops_dashboard.dash_title = 'OPERATIONS DASHBOARD' # MOVE 'OPERATIONS DASHBOARD' TO DASH-TITLE
    ws.ws_ops_dashboard.dash_trans_count = ws.ws_total_trans_count # MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT TO DASH-TRANS-COUNT
    ws.ws_ops_dashboard.dash_avg_response = ws.ws_avg_response_time # MOVE WS-AVG-RESPONSE-TIME TO DASH-AVG-RESPONSE
    ws.ws_ops_dashboard.dash_error_rate = ws.ws_error_rate # MOVE WS-ERROR-RATE TO DASH-ERROR-RATE
    ws.ws_ops_dashboard.dash_sla_pct = ws.ws_sla_compliance # MOVE WS-SLA-COMPLIANCE TO DASH-SLA-PCT
    # WRITE DASHBOARD-RECORD FROM WS-OPS-DASHBOARD
    print(f"Writing operations dashboard: {ws.ws_ops_dashboard}")

def create_risk_dashboard(ws: WSRecords) -> None:
    """Original COBOL line: 21430-CREATE-RISK-DASHBOARD."""
    ws.ws_risk_dashboard = RiskDashboard()
    ws.ws_risk_dashboard.dash_title = 'RISK DASHBOARD' # MOVE 'RISK DASHBOARD' TO DASH

def current_date():
    """Replicates COBOL's FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE.

    For simplicity, returns current datetime as a string.
    In a real implementation, this might return a specific format.
    """
    return datetime.now().isoformat()

def min(a: Decimal, b: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Replicates COBOL's FUNCTION MIN.

    Returns the smaller of two Decimal values.
    """
    return a if a <= b else b

def perform_37235_log_recon_exception(ws_gl_account: str, ws_recon_diff: Decimal, recon_exception_record, ws_recon_exception: ReconException) -> None:
    """COBOL: 
           WS-GL-CONTROL-BAL - WS-SUBLEDGER-TOTAL
           IF WS-RECON-DIFF NOT = ZEROES
              PERFORM 37235-LOG-RECON-EXCEPTION
           END-IF."""
    if ws_recon_diff != Decimal("0"):
        perform_37235_log_recon_exception_inner(ws_gl_account, ws_recon_diff, recon_exception_record, ws_recon_exception)

def perform_37235_log_recon_exception_inner(ws_gl_account: str, ws_recon_diff: Decimal, recon_exception_record, ws_recon_exception: ReconException) -> None:
    """COBOL: 37235-LOG-RECON-EXCEPTION.
           INITIALIZE WS-RECON-EXCEPTION
           MOVE WS-GL-ACCOUNT TO RECON-EXC-ACCOUNT
           MOVE WS-RECON-DIFF TO RECON-EXC-DIFF
           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO RECON-EXC-DATE
           WRITE RECON-EXCEPTION-RECORD FROM WS-RECON-EXCEPTION."""
    ws_recon_exception = ReconException()
    ws_recon_exception.recon_exc_account = ws_gl_account
    ws_recon_exception.recon_exc_diff = ws_recon_diff
    ws_recon_exception.recon_exc_date = date.today().isoformat()
    recon_exception_record = ws_recon_exception  # Assuming writing means assigning for this context

def perform_37300_intercompany_recon(ws_ic_array, intercompany_file, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_ic_count: int, ws_ic_balance: ICBalance, ic_from_entity, ic_to_entity, ic_amount, ws_ic_idx: int, ws_search_from: str, ws_search_to: str, ws_ic_idx2: int, ws_ic_diff: Decimal, ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec: ICDiffRec) -> None:
    """COBOL: 37300-INTERCOMPANY-RECON.
           PERFORM 37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES
           PERFORM 37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS
           PERFORM 37330-REPORT-IC-DIFFERENCES."""
    ws_eof_flag, ws_ic_count, ws_ic_array, ws_ic_balance, intercompany_file = perform_37310_load_ic_balances(ws_ic_array, intercompany_file, ws_eof_flag, ws_ic_count, ws_ic_balance)
    ws_ic_array, ic_from_entity, ic_to_entity, ic_amount, ws_ic_idx, ws_search_from, ws_search_to, ws_ic_idx2, ws_ic_diff, ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec = perform_37320_match_ic_pairs(ws_ic_array, ws_ic_count, ic_from_entity, ic_to_entity, ic_amount, ws_ic_idx, ws_search_from, ws_search_to, ws_ic_idx2, ws_ic_diff, ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec)
    perform_37330_report_ic_differences()
    return ws_ic_array, ic_from_entity, ic_to_entity, ic_amount

def perform_37310_load_ic_balances(ws_ic_array, intercompany_file, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_ic_count: int, ws_ic_balance: ICBalance) -> tuple[str, int, list, ICBalance, list]:
    """COBOL: 37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-IC-COUNT
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ INTERCOMPANY-FILE INTO WS-IC-BALANCE
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    ADD 1 TO WS-IC-COUNT
                    MOVE WS-IC-BALANCE TO 
                       WS-IC-ARRAY(WS-IC-COUNT)
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    ws_ic_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_ic_balance = intercompany_file.pop(0)  # Assuming file is a list
            ws_ic_count += 1
            ws_ic_array.append(ws_ic_balance)
        except IndexError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    return ws_eof_flag, ws_ic_count, ws_ic_array, ws_ic_balance, intercompany_file

def perform_37320_match_ic_pairs(ws_ic_array, ws_ic_count: int, ic_from_entity, ic_to_entity, ic_amount, ws_ic_idx: int, ws_search_from: str, ws_search_to: str, ws_ic_idx2: int, ws_ic_diff: Decimal, ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec: ICDiffRec) -> tuple[list, list, list, list, int, str, str, int, Decimal, list, ICDiffRec]:
    """COBOL: 37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS.
           PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
              UNTIL WS-IC-IDX > WS-IC-COUNT
              PERFORM 37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART
           END-PERFORM."""
    ws_ic_idx = 1
    while ws_ic_idx <= ws_ic_count:
        ic_from_entity, ic_to_entity, ic_amount, ws_search_from, ws_search_to, ws_ic_idx2, ws_ic_diff, ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec = perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart(ws_ic_array, ws_ic_idx, ws_ic_count, ic_from_entity, ic_to_entity, ic_amount, ws_search_from, ws_search_to, ws_ic_idx2, ws_ic_diff, ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec)
        ws_ic_idx += 1
    return ws_ic_array, ic_from_entity, ic_to_entity, ic_amount, ws_ic_idx, ws_search_from, ws_search_to, ws_ic_idx2, ws_ic_diff, ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec

def perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart(ws_ic_array, ws_ic_idx: int, ws_ic_count: int, ic_from_entity, ic_to_entity, ic_amount, ws_search_from: str, ws_search_to: str, ws_ic_idx2: int, ws_ic_diff: Decimal, ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec: ICDiffRec) -> tuple[list, list, list, str, str, int, Decimal, list, ICDiffRec]:
    """COBOL: 37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART.
           MOVE IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-FROM
           MOVE IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-TO
           PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX2 FROM 1 BY 1
              UNTIL WS-IC-IDX2 > WS-IC-COUNT
              IF IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-TO
                 IF IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-FROM
                    COMPUTE WS-IC-DIFF = 
                       IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX) + 
                       IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX2)
                    IF WS-IC-DIFF NOT = ZEROES
                       PERFORM 37326-LOG-IC-DIFF
                    END-IF
                    EXIT PERFORM
                 END-IF
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM."""
    ws_search_from = ws_ic_array[ws_ic_idx-1].ic_from_entity
    ws_search_to = ws_ic_array[ws_ic_idx-1].ic_to_entity
    ws_ic_idx2 = 1
    while ws_ic_idx2 <= ws_ic_count:
        if ws_ic_array[ws_ic_idx2-1].ic_from_entity == ws_search_to:
            if ws_ic_array[ws_ic_idx2-1].ic_to_entity == ws_search_from:
                ws_ic_diff = ws_ic_array[ws_ic_idx-1].ic_amount + ws_ic_array[ws_ic_idx2-1].ic_amount
                if ws_ic_diff != Decimal("0"):
                    ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec = perform_37326_log_ic_diff(ws_search_from, ws_search_to, ws_ic_diff, ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec)
                break  # EXIT PERFORM
        ws_ic_idx2 += 1
    return ic_from_entity, ic_to_entity, ic_amount, ws_search_from, ws_search_to, ws_ic_idx2, ws_ic_diff, ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec

def perform_37326_log_ic_diff(ws_search_from: str, ws_search_to: str, ws_ic_diff: Decimal, ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec: ICDiffRec) -> tuple[list, ICDiffRec]:
    """COBOL: 37326-LOG-IC-DIFF.
           INITIALIZE WS-IC-DIFF-REC
           MOVE WS-SEARCH-FROM TO ICD-FROM
           MOVE WS-SEARCH-TO TO ICD-TO
           MOVE WS-IC-DIFF TO ICD-AMOUNT
           WRITE IC-DIFF-RECORD FROM WS-IC-DIFF-REC."""
    ws_ic_diff_rec = ICDiffRec()
    ws_ic_diff_rec.icd_from = ws_search_from
    ws_ic_diff_rec.icd_to = ws_search_to
    ws_ic_diff_rec.icd_amount = ws_ic_diff
    ic_diff_record = ws_ic_diff_rec  # Assuming writing means assigning for this context
    return ic_diff_record, ws_ic_diff_rec

def perform_37330_report_ic_differences() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37330-REPORT-IC-DIFFERENCES.
           DISPLAY 'INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE'."""
    print('INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE')

def perform_37400_nostro_recon(nostro_statement_file, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_nostro_count: int, ws_nostro_item) -> None:
    """COBOL: 37400-NOSTRO-RECON.
           PERFORM 37410-LOAD-NOSTRO-STATEMENT
           PERFORM 37420-MATCH-NOSTRO-ENTRIES
           PERFORM 37430-GENERATE-NOSTRO-REPORT."""
    ws_eof_flag, ws_nostro_count, nostro_statement_file, ws_nostro_item = perform_37410_load_nostro_statement(nostro_statement_file, ws_eof_flag, ws_nostro_count, ws_nostro_item)
    perform_37420_match_nostro_entries()
    perform_37430_generate_nostro_report()

def perform_37410_load_nostro_statement(nostro_statement_file, ws_eof_flag: str, ws_nostro_count: int, ws_nostro_item) -> tuple[str, int, list, str]:
    """COBOL: 37410-LOAD-NOSTRO-STATEMENT.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NOSTRO-COUNT
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ NOSTRO-STATEMENT-FILE INTO WS-NOSTRO-ITEM
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    ADD 1 TO WS-NOSTRO-COUNT
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    ws_nostro_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_nostro_item = nostro_statement_file.pop(0)  # Assuming file is a list
            ws_nostro_count += 1
        except IndexError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    return ws_eof_flag, ws_nostro_count, nostro_statement_file, ws_nostro_item

def perform_37420_match_nostro_entries() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37420-MATCH-NOSTRO-ENTRIES.
           DISPLAY 'MATCHING NOSTRO ENTRIES'."""
    print('MATCHING NOSTRO ENTRIES')

def perform_37430_generate_nostro_report() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37430-GENERATE-NOSTRO-REPORT.
           DISPLAY 'NOSTRO RECONCILIATION COMPLETE'."""
    print('NOSTRO RECONCILIATION COMPLETE')

def perform_38000_audit_trail(ws_user_id: str, ws_action_type: str, ws_session_id: str, ws_table_name: str, ws_record_key: str, ws_old_value: str, ws_new_value: str, ws_event_type: str, ws_end_of_month: str, ws_audit_record: AuditRecord, audit_record, ws_archive_date: str, audit_file, archive_audit_record) -> None:
    """COBOL: 38000-AUDIT-TRAIL.
           PERFORM 38100-LOG-USER-ACTION
           PERFORM 38200-LOG-DATA-CHANGE
           PERFORM 38300-LOG-SYSTEM-EVENT
           PERFORM 38400-ARCHIVE-AUDIT-LOGS."""
    audit_record, ws_audit_record = perform_38100_log_user_action(ws_user_id, ws_action_type, ws_session_id, ws_audit_record, audit_record)
    audit_record, ws_audit_record = perform_38200_log_data_change(ws_user_id, ws_table_name, ws_record_key, ws_old_value, ws_new_value, ws_audit_record, audit_record)
    audit_record, ws_audit_record = perform_38300_log_system_event(ws_event_type, ws_audit_record, audit_record)
    perform_38400_archive_audit_logs(ws_end_of_month, ws_archive_date, audit_file, archive_audit_record, ws_audit_record)

def perform_38100_log_user_action(ws_user_id: str, ws_action_type: str, ws_session_id: str, ws_audit_record: AuditRecord, audit_record) -> tuple[list, AuditRecord]:
    """COBOL: 38100-LOG-USER-ACTION.
           INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
           COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
           MOVE WS-USER-ID TO WS-AUDIT-USER
           MOVE WS-ACTION-TYPE TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
           MOVE WS-SESSION-ID TO WS-AUDIT-SESSION-ID
           WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD."""
    import random
    ws_audit_record = AuditRecord()
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_id = Decimal(str(random.random() * 99999999999))
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_timestamp = date.today().isoformat()
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_user = ws_user_id
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_action = ws_action_type
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_session_id = ws_session_id
    audit_record = ws_audit_record
    return audit_record, ws_audit_record  # Assuming writing means assigning for this context

def perform_38200_log_data_change(ws_user_id: str, ws_table_name: str, ws_record_key: str, ws_old_value: str, ws_new_value: str, ws_audit_record: AuditRecord, audit_record) -> tuple[list, AuditRecord]:
    """COBOL: 38200-LOG-DATA-CHANGE.
           INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
           COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
           MOVE WS-USER-ID TO WS-AUDIT-USER
           MOVE 'UPDATE' TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
           MOVE WS-TABLE-NAME TO WS-AUDIT-TABLE
           MOVE WS-RECORD-KEY TO WS-AUDIT-KEY
           MOVE WS-OLD-VALUE TO WS-AUDIT-OLD-VALUE
           MOVE WS-NEW-VALUE TO WS-AUDIT-NEW-VALUE
           WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD."""
    import random
    ws_audit_record = AuditRecord()
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_id = Decimal(str(random.random() * 99999999999))
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_timestamp = date.today().isoformat()
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_user = ws_user_id
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_action = 'UPDATE'
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_table = ws_table_name
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_key = ws_record_key
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_old_value = ws_old_value
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_new_value = ws_new_value
    audit_record = ws_audit_record
    return audit_record, ws_audit_record  # Assuming writing means assigning for this context

def perform_38300_log_system_event(ws_event_type: str, ws_audit_record: AuditRecord, audit_record) -> tuple[list, AuditRecord]:
    """COBOL: 38300-LOG-SYSTEM-EVENT.
           INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
           COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
           MOVE 'SYSTEM' TO WS-AUDIT-USER
           MOVE WS-EVENT-TYPE TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
           WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD."""
    import random
    ws_audit_record = AuditRecord()
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_id = Decimal(str(random.random() * 99999999999))
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_timestamp = date.today().isoformat()
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_user = 'SYSTEM'
    ws_audit_record.ws_audit_action = ws_event_type
    audit_record = ws_audit_record
    return audit_record, ws_audit_record  # Assuming writing means assigning for this context

def perform_38400_archive_audit_logs(ws_end_of_month: str, ws_archive_date: str, audit_file, archive_audit_record, ws_audit_record: AuditRecord) -> None:
    """COBOL: 38400-ARCHIVE-AUDIT-LOGS.
           IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
              PERFORM 38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE
              PERFORM 38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE
           END-IF."""
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        perform_38410_move_to_archive(ws_archive_date, audit_file, archive_audit_record, ws_audit_record)
        perform_38420_compress_archive()

def perform_38410_move_to_archive(ws_archive_date: str, audit_file, archive_audit_record, ws_audit_record: AuditRecord) -> None:
    """COBOL: 38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE.
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
              READ AUDIT-FILE INTO WS-AUDIT-RECORD
                 AT END
                    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
                 NOT AT END
                    IF WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP < WS-ARCHIVE-DATE
                       WRITE ARCHIVE-AUDIT-RECORD 
                          FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD
                       DELETE AUDIT-FILE
                    END-IF
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM
           MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            ws_audit_record = audit_file.pop(0)  # Assuming file is a list
            if ws_audit_record.ws_audit_timestamp < ws_archive_date:
                archive_audit_record.append(ws_audit_record)  # Assuming writing means appending
                #DELETE AUDIT-FILE (Not implemented as deleting the whole list)
        except IndexError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def perform_38420_compress_archive() -> None:
    """COBOL: 38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE.
           DISPLAY 'COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE'."""
    print('COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE')

def perform_39000_performance_monitoring(ws_cpu_utilization: int, ws_memory_utilization: int, ws_io_wait_time: int, ws_io_threshold: int, ws_trans_count: int, ws_elapsed_seconds: int, ws_total_response_time: int, ws_response_threshold: int, ws_min_tps_threshold: int, ws_cpu_alert: str, ws_memory_alert: str, ws_io_alert: str, ws_tps: Decimal, ws_avg_response: Decimal, ws_perf_degraded: str, ws_throughput_low: str, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> None:
    """COBOL: 39000-PERFORMANCE-MONITORING.
           PERFORM 39100-COLLECT-METRICS
           PERFORM 39200-ANALYZE-PERFORMANCE
           PERFORM 39300-GENERATE-ALERTS
           PERFORM 39400-OPTIMIZE-RESOURCES."""
    ws_cpu_utilization, ws_memory_utilization, ws_io_wait_time, ws_trans_count, ws_elapsed_seconds, ws_total_response_time, ws_cpu_alert, ws_memory_alert, ws_io_alert, ws_tps, ws_avg_response = perform_39100_collect_metrics(ws_cpu_utilization, ws_memory_utilization, ws_io_wait_time, ws_io_threshold, ws_trans_count, ws_elapsed_seconds, ws_total_response_time, ws_cpu_alert, ws_memory_alert, ws_io_alert)
    ws_avg_response, ws_tps, ws_response_threshold, ws_min_tps_threshold, ws_perf_degraded, ws_throughput_low = perform_39200_analyze_performance(ws_avg_response, ws_tps, ws_response_threshold, ws_min_tps_threshold, ws_perf_degraded, ws_throughput_low)
    ws_cpu_alert, ws_memory_alert, ws_perf_degraded, ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject = perform_39300_generate_alerts(ws_cpu_alert, ws_memory_alert, ws_perf_degraded, ws_cpu_utilization, ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)
    perform_39400_optimize_resources(ws_perf_degraded)
    return ws_cpu_alert, ws_memory_alert, ws_perf_degraded, ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject

def perform_39100_collect_metrics(ws_cpu_utilization: int, ws_memory_utilization: int, ws_io_wait_time: int, ws_io_threshold: int, ws_trans_count: int, ws_elapsed_seconds: int, ws_total_response_time: int, ws_cpu_alert: str, ws_memory_alert: str, ws_io_alert: str, ws_tps: Decimal, ws_avg_response: Decimal) -> tuple[int, int, int, int, int, int, str, str, str, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """COBOL: 39100-COLLECT-METRICS.
           PERFORM 39110-CPU-METRICS
           PERFORM 39120-MEMORY-METRICS
           PERFORM 39130-IO-METRICS
           PERFORM 39140-TRANSACTION-METRICS."""
    ws_cpu_utilization, ws_cpu_alert = perform_39110_cpu_metrics(ws_cpu_utilization, ws_cpu_alert)
    ws_memory_utilization, ws_memory_alert = perform_39120_memory_metrics(ws_memory_utilization, ws_memory_alert)
    ws_io_wait_time, ws_io_threshold, ws_io_alert = perform_39130_io_metrics(ws_io_wait_time, ws_io_threshold, ws_io_alert)
    ws_trans_count, ws_elapsed_seconds, ws_total_response_time, ws_tps, ws_avg_response = perform_39140_transaction_metrics(ws_trans_count, ws_elapsed_seconds, ws_total_response_time, ws_tps, ws_avg_response)
    return ws_cpu_utilization, ws_memory_utilization, ws_io_wait_time, ws_trans_count, ws_elapsed_seconds, ws_total_response_time, ws_cpu_alert, ws_memory_alert, ws_io_alert, ws_tps, ws_avg_response

def perform_39110_cpu_metrics(ws_cpu_utilization: int, ws_cpu_alert: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """COBOL: 39110-CPU-METRICS.
           CALL 'GETCPU' USING WS-CPU-UTILIZATION
           IF WS-CPU-UTILIZATION > 80
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CPU-ALERT
           END-IF."""
    # Assume CALL 'GETCPU' returns CPU utilization
    # For simulation, using a random number
    import random
    ws_cpu_utilization = random.randint(0, 100)  # Simulate CPU utilization
    if ws_cpu_utilization > 80:
        ws_cpu_alert = 'Y'
    return ws_cpu_utilization, ws_cpu_alert

def perform_39120_memory_metrics(ws_memory_utilization: int, ws_memory_alert: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """COBOL: 39120-MEMORY-METRICS.
           CALL 'GETMEM' USING WS-MEMORY-UTILIZATION
           IF WS-MEMORY-UTILIZATION > 85
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MEMORY-ALERT
           END-IF."""
    # Assume CALL 'GETMEM' returns memory utilization
    # For simulation, using a random number
    import random
    ws_memory_utilization = random.randint(0, 100)  # Simulate memory utilization
    if ws_memory_utilIZATION > 85:
        ws_memory_alert = 'Y'
